"Fan'er..." Bai Shuyue was completely dumbfounded. She had never imagined that the destruction of the vast Profound Yellow Realm was merely a casual act of a true immortal.

"You shouldn't think about revenge now. Once we reach a safe place, let's focus on cultivating. See if your cultivation restrictions have disappeared? With your talent, perhaps you can even surpass that woman and achieve Transcendence before her. Then, you can help us get revenge!" the Boundless Wall said fiercely.

Bai Shuyue, however, turned a deaf ear, still shaken by the destruction of the Profound Yellow Realm. In her past reincarnations, it was at most her own death. The destruction of an entire world was the first time. Half a day later, Bai Shuyue finally recovered. Temporarily suppressing the urge to self-terminate and restart, Bai Shuyue forced herself to calm down and began to comprehend the Great Dao in the dark starry sky.

A moment later... "Such clear Great Dao!" Bai Shuyue exclaimed in shock. If comprehending the Great Dao in the Profound Yellow Realm was like looking through layers of misty fog, then comprehending it in this sea of stars was like viewing an incredibly clear painting up close. As if heaven and earth had suddenly become clear. "The difference is this huge?"

Somewhat surprised and delighted, Bai Shuyue immersed herself in the unprecedented comprehension. At this moment, she keenly sensed a faint will in this sea of stars! "This is..."

"The consciousness of the Star Sea?" Bai Shuyue's heart trembled. This was a novel experience for her, something she had never encountered before. She had never imagined that the vast Star Sea beyond the Profound Yellow Realm could actually possess its own consciousness. Naturally, Bai Shuyue wanted to establish a deeper connection with it.

"Go there!" Bai Shuyue pointed to a spot in the darkness. "What for..." the Boundless Wall grumbled unhappily. However, at Bai Shuyue's repeated insistence, it changed direction. As they approached the center of the Star Sea, in Bai Shuyue's eyes, the originally pitch-black Star Sea gradually brightened. She also keenly sensed a burgeoning vitality within this dead Star Sea! However, this wisp of vitality was still extremely weak, so weak that any further disturbance could extinguish it in its cradle.

The fragile life, like an infant, evoked pity and tenderness in Bai Shuyue. A resonance... It was precisely this peculiar emotion that allowed Bai Shuyue to make her first contact with the True Intent of the Origin of the Uttermost Dark Star Sea.

The originally vibrant, boundless starry sky. Everything was peaceful. However, dark clouds occasionally flashed by—disturbances from the upper realm. Meteors from the Immortal Realm became increasingly frequent. But they had no significant impact on the survival of the Star Sea itself. Until that day. The immortal Dao collapsed, the Star Sea was destroyed, and calamity spread.

A figure, whose face and details were unclear, becoming increasingly faint. It completely disappeared. Only the sound of its light laughter echoed in the Star Sea, on the verge of widespread catastrophe. "What does it have to do with me..." "What does it have to do with me..." "Hahahaha..."

... As if witnessing baby Li Fan being strangled in his cradle, Bai Shuyue's heart suddenly surged with anger. It made her even disregard the gap between immortal and mortal techniques. Towards the direction where the figure disappeared, she questioned, "Who are you!"

"Who are you?" Bai Shuyue's voice echoed continuously in the memories of the Star Sea's True Intent. Like ripples upon ripples. The figure that had completely disappeared actually reappeared, stimulated by the continuous reverberations! No longer laughing wildly, it simply looked at Bai Shuyue. Its expression was somewhat enigmatic.

"Interesting." A faint voice sounded, which fell upon Bai Shuyue's ears like thunder. "This... is not in the memories of the Star Sea. It's speaking to me?" "How is that possible?" The illusory existence in her memory had actually become reality. This was a divine ability Bai Shuyue could not even imagine.

Like being doused with a basin of cold water, her anger instantly extinguished. Afterward, boundless fear arose. She wanted to escape from the Star Sea's memories. However, she found she could not move. As if reliving the day the Star Sea calamity struck, the figure approached Bai Shuyue. It attempted to gently stroke her.

Just then... "Ah, ah, ah! It's you, you villain!" the Boundless Wall's furious voice suddenly erupted. A plaque, like a flying sword, tore through space and time, smashing into the figure's face.

Seeing the Saint Monarch's Palace plaque, the figure's expression fluctuated slightly. It cautiously scanned its surroundings. Discovering that the attacker was merely a tile, it relaxed. Before it could speak, several streams of light, like a meteor shower, arrived. All targeted the same point. During their flight, they erupted into flames. Upon closing in, they detonated with a resounding boom.

Amidst the series of explosions, the illusory space shattered into countless pieces, like glass dropped on the ground. "A pity." "Too far away." As its form vanished, it sighed softly.

Chapter 1625: Seeing He Ganxian Again

"With your talent, I can confidently entrust it to you." Although it said this, the Boundless Wall's large eyeball still looked somewhat reluctant. It slowly and sluggishly revealed the "[Seize]" technique. Bai Shuyue saw its behavior and felt strange. It had not even concealed the Saint Monarch's Palace plaque, so there was no reason for it to hide a mere True Immortal Seal Script. It was only after Bai Shuyue deciphered the "[Seize]" technique that she understood why. With flowing strokes, a golden True Immortal Seal Script instantly formed in the air. The Boundless Wall's eyes flickered, having already retreated far away before the "[Seize]" technique appeared.

"Miss Bai! We agreed that the [Seize] technique was to deal with that brat with the golden hair. Why are you using it on me now?" Bai Shuyue did not answer, merely waving her other hand. The "[Restrain]" technique in the air instantly appeared. Like two suns shining together, they illuminated the nine heavens! The Boundless Wall's eyes showed panic as it retreated even further.

"The '[Restrain]' and '[Seize]' characters both belong to the unique seal scripts of the Immortal Court. They were special means used by the Immortal Court to suppress the myriad immortals. When combined, they are like immortal soldiers and generals setting up a formation to search for enemies, their power being several times greater than any individual character." "By casting these two talismans, I can not only suppress and plunder the Boundless Wall but also plunder its private collection. The Saint Monarch's Palace plaque..." For a moment, Bai Shuyue did consider such a thought. However, in recent lives, memories of spending time with the Boundless Wall flashed through her mind. She ultimately did not act. "Humans are not insensible beings; how can one be without feelings? In this life, it has shown me kindness. I cannot repay kindness with enmity. When the next life begins anew, with no past ties and debts between us, it will be time enough." Thinking this, the '[Restrain]' and '[Seize]' characters in the air hovered gently and then dissipated. Bai Shuyue looked at the Boundless Wall, still hiding far away, and chuckled, "It's just my divine ability at its nascent stage; I was merely testing its feel. Why are you so nervous? We are life-and-death allies; how could I possibly harm you!" The Boundless Wall observed for a while longer, and seeing that Bai Shuyue showed no signs of hostility, it finally relaxed. It said smugly, "I was merely testing you. Since I dared to teach you these two characters, naturally I have a way to break them. If you had truly attacked me, hmph..." Whether its words were true or not, Bai Shuyue silently bore them in mind.

"With my divine ability formed, it's time to seek revenge on that brat. He caused us such a mess; it's simply outrageous!" the Boundless Wall said excitedly. In its view, being humiliated by a mortal was absolutely unacceptable. It had to reclaim its honor, or its title of "Before the Saint Monarch's Gate" would be meaningless! Bai Shuyue, however, remained calm. "There's no need to seek him out. We just need to wait here, like a hunter waiting for prey." Bai Shuyue's premonition was not wrong. Ou Shangtian seemed to possess a special tracking method; even from a great distance, he could easily pinpoint their location. After ambushing them for less than three days, Ou Shangtian's aura was faintly detected on the horizon, rapidly approaching. However, strangely, just as he was about to enter the ambush zone, Ou Shangtian stopped without warning. After a brief pause, he turned and left without hesitation.

"Such sharp intuition!" Bai Shuyue was greatly surprised and no longer hesitated. The two True Immortal Seal Scripts were unleashed simultaneously. Myriad golden chains and a pale, massive hand suddenly appeared in the sky, trapping Ou Shangtian within.

"Invert the Heavens and Earth!"

A furious roar resounded, attempting to scatter the sealing power of the True Immortal Seal Scripts. However, seemingly affected by the "[Seize]" technique, the immortal bone within Ou Shangtian's body rapidly retracted its golden light. His divine power vanished instantly, as if it had never existed. Empowered by immortal bone, Ou Shangtian became ordinary! Like falling from the clouds to the earth, having lost the enhancement of the two immortal artifacts, Ou Shangtian was, without suspense, successfully suppressed and captured by Bai Shuyue.

"Good, good, good! I'll let you chase again!" The Boundless Wall, which had disappeared during the battle, now revealed itself triumphantly, wielding dozens of tendrils and incessantly tormenting the tightly bound Ou Shangtian. "I'll slowly dig out your immortal bone, inch by inch, later!" the Boundless Wall whispered with a hidden laugh, its single eye pressed close to Ou Shangtian's face. "Kill me or dismember me, as you wish! Don't think I'll fear you two wicked thieves!" Ou Shangtian, unfazed, cursed them. The Boundless Wall flew into a rage, and five tendrils plunged into Ou Shangtian's body, churning vigorously. Ou Shangtian nearly convulsed in pain, yet he endured it, not uttering a single sound, let alone begging for mercy. This stubbornness only further enraged the Boundless Wall. It resolved to employ more brutal methods, determined to make the mortal boy submit.

Compared to this, Bai Shuyue frowned slightly while watching Ou Shangtian's display. A wave of emotion washed over her. "Wicked thieves... I never thought that one day, others would call me by such a name." "Speaking of which, we were in the wrong initially, accidentally killing his father. Seeking revenge for a loved one is only natural. Moreover, this youth, despite suffering such cruel punishment, remains steadfast." "Yes, he's a good child, just like Fan'er." "Let's just give him a quick end." With this thought, Bai Shuyue interrupted the Boundless Wall's torment. The tendrils instantly drained Ou Shangtian's lifespan, and after extracting the two immortal artifacts from his body, Bai Shuyue intended to leave. To find a safe place and meticulously study the spoils she had obtained. However, she hadn't flown far when a strong sense of unease surged within her. She scanned her surroundings with suspicion but found no source of the unease. Judging by the Boundless Wall's reaction, it was clearly not her imagination.

"Hurry and escape! We seem to be targeted by something!" Bai Shuyue's expression turned grim as she fled with all her might, but the unease in her heart only intensified. Finally, nine golden needles descended from the sky, instantly sealing off Bai Shuyue from all directions! After a moment of stunned silence, Bai Shuyue reacted. She intended to use the True Immortal Seal Script again to defend herself. Unexpectedly... A pale hand silently appeared above her head. It gently pressed a long golden needle into her Baihui acupoint. The golden needle entered her body, piercing her skull. Her entire being seemed frozen, her expression caught between shock and panic. Not only could her entire body not move, but even her thoughts were sealed in ice. The Boundless Wall, disregarding any camaraderie, let out a startled cry and prepared to flee. But it was interrupted by a light hum. Two fingers gently pinched the jade tile. The Boundless Wall trembled and struggled incessantly, its jade surface revealing the blurred outline of the Saint Monarch's Palace plaque.

"Hmm?" After a voice filled with both surprise and delight, the Heavenly Physician slowly revealed himself. He quickly tapped the surface of the Boundless Wall several times. The Saint Monarch's Palace plaque, which had been trying to break free, trembled slightly and then fell silent again. The Boundless Wall itself seemed to have fallen into a slumber, just like Bai Shuyue. Looking at the temporarily sealed person and artifact before him, the Heavenly Physician pondered. Not long ago, he had been searching near the remnant Great Wall beneath the high walls for suitable fragments of cultivation worlds. Unexpectedly, he suddenly sensed fluctuations in True Immortal Seal Script energies from the Profound Yellow Realm. Although it wasn't the same one that caused the catastrophe in the Heavenly Law Realm years ago, the Heavenly Physician decided to return and investigate. After all, the Profound Yellow Realm was of great importance, and no mistakes could be tolerated. Later, sensing the appearance of a second True Immortal Seal Script, the Heavenly Physician's return speeded up. "The Profound Yellow Realm has been peaceful for a long time. Now, within a short period, two True Immortal Seal Scripts have appeared... What exactly happened?" The Heavenly Physician was filled with doubt and uncertainty. Upon arriving near the Profound Yellow Realm, the two True Immortal Seal Scripts erupted once more. The Heavenly Physician followed the trail, pinpointing the source of the True Immortal Seal Scripts. "This jade tile seems to be an item from the Immortal Realm. And that plaque on the jade tile just now..." "The Tai Yi Palace?" The Heavenly Physician's expression turned grave. Although the Boundless Wall's origin was extraordinary, the Heavenly Physician's gaze remained fixed on Bai Shuyue. "In the moment before I sealed her, this woman's thoughts were rather strange." "On the surface, she seemed shocked and fearful, but deep down..." The Heavenly Physician frowned, flying around the immobilized Bai Shuyue several times. "Fearless?" "Does she have some other reliance?" The more the Heavenly Physician observed, the more he felt his intuition was correct. Golden needles of various lengths were arranged, and the Heavenly Physician prepared to conduct a more thorough examination of Bai Shuyue. "If the Heavenly Demon were still here, this wouldn't be so troublesome. Alas, I need his power to suppress the transmission of the Dao." Thinking this, the Heavenly Physician held the needle, preparing to pierce Bai Shuyue's forehead. Just as the golden needle was about to penetrate, a crisp snapping sound echoed through the heavens and earth. The Heavenly Physician's movement instantly halted. His expression, like Bai Shuyue's before him, became frozen in time. In fact, it wasn't just the Heavenly Physician; the entire Profound Yellow Realm fell into a state of stagnation with that light sound. Li Fan's figure appeared on the scene. Looking at the scene before him, he shook his head slightly. "Fortunately, I was vigilant back then. Otherwise, this would be the end of the line." "But it's alright. Auntie, I'll help you!" Li Fan took the Heavenly Physician's hand and forcefully thrust the uninserted golden needle into him. In an instant, many scenarios were filled in. Then... "True and False!" The Saint Creation Plan, the twenty-eighth generation! Bai Shuyue woke up from her nightmare, unable to recover for a long time. This time, the shock she felt was even more intense than during her first "rebirth." She subconsciously touched her head, as if the pain there had not yet dissipated. After the golden needle pierced her, her consciousness had frozen, so she did not know how she had died. However, she still retained some vague images in her mind. Treated like an object, golden needles filled her entire body. And later, she was dismembered into countless pieces... Even recalling the faint residual memories of the previous life, Bai Shuyue's face turned instantly deathly pale. "Ugh..." Instinctively bending over, she vomited uncontrollably. Her body trembled violently, unable to stop. Months passed, and she still hadn't recovered. After all, despite Bai Shuyue's twenty-plus layerings of spirituality, making her incredibly talented, her experiences could not match her current aptitude. It was fine if she died normally before, but after encountering the Heavenly Physician in the previous life and undergoing such inhuman torture, Bai Shuyue became terrified. She did not want to, nor dared to, nor wished to venture into the Profound Yellow Realm again. "The Profound Yellow Realm is simply too dangerous and terrifying." "Living as a mortal emperor in Great Xuan, staying put, wouldn't that be good too." Once this thought arose, it spread like wildfire, impossible to extinguish. Bai Shuyue dared not even speculate about the true identity of the figure who appeared at the end of the previous life. She chose to bury her head in the sand and escape. Li Fan's actions were within Bai Shuyue's expectations. After all, Li Fan had personally implanted the images in Bai Shuyue's mind. "This is her inner demon." "As long as she overcomes this hurdle, her path ahead will be clear, and nothing will hinder her. Even if she encounters any danger in the future, she will not despair but will still press forward." "And if she cannot overcome it..." "Heh heh, with my help, how could she possibly not overcome it?" To resolve Bai Shuyue's inner demon, Li Fan made a dramatic appearance. Bai Shuyue was terrified. During the months she was bedridden, it was Li Fan, who acted completely out of character, who diligently cared for her, without complaint. Bai Shuyue's complexion slowly improved. However, Li Fan, overworked and exhausted, grew thinner day by day. Yet, he still forced a smile, not wanting Bai Shuyue to worry. Until... "Auntie, lunch is ready. I learned a new dish, bean rice and wild greens. Come and try it quickly." Li Fan, holding a wooden tray filled with dishes, entered Bai Shuyue's room. Bai Shuyue looked at him, her heart aching, and was about to speak. She saw Li Fan, staggering with unsteady steps, fall to the ground. The shattering of the porcelain bowl sounded like thunder, striking Bai Shuyue's heart. "Fan'er!" The roles reversed. Now, it was Li Fan who was bedridden. "This is exhaustion taking its toll; the illness of natural decline has struck prematurely." Bai Shuyue, having mastered medicine, immediately recognized the cause of Li Fan's illness. Guilt flashed in her heart. "If I hadn't been so flustered and fearful, unwilling to pay attention to the world around me, Fan'er wouldn't have ended up like this in this life. He's only twenty years old!" Accompanying Li Fan through the final period of his life, Bai Shuyue stood before Li Fan's grave six months later, her expression grief-stricken. It had been a long time since she had experienced the sorrow of an elder burying a younger one. And after going through this ordeal, Bai Shuyue finally understood that even after so many reincarnations, the seemingly weakened familial affection had not diminished in the slightest! Li Fan was still as dear to her as her own flesh and blood. She was still the only relative Li Fan could rely on in the world! "Fan'er, it's okay." "Auntie will come find you now." At Li Fan's grave, Bai Shuyue took her own life. The twenty-ninth generation of the Saint Creation Plan began.

Chapter 1626: Faceless Virtuous Immortal

"Ah, ah, ah, this time it's a real heavy loss..." The wailings of the Boundless Wall continuously echoed in Bai Shuyue's ears.

Like striking copper bells, they slowly brought back Bai Shuyue's lost divine soul.

As if waking from a deep dream, she cleared her mind from the terrifying experience she had just endured.

Just as she was about to speak, she suddenly found that she couldn't speak at all!

"Wuwuwu..." Terrified, Bai Shuyue instinctively reached out to touch her face, wanting to see what was happening.

It was better not to touch, but the moment she did, her heart was even more shocked.

She quickly used the Water Mirror Technique to check, and this time she finally confirmed.

Her facial features had all disappeared!

Even the curve and appearance of her entire face were vaguely similar to the figure she encountered in the memories of the Star Sea Origin True Intent just now!

"It's just losing your face, what's there to be afraid of? At least you saved your life." The comforting voice of the Boundless Wall came in time, making Bai Shuyue feel a little relieved.

"It's really bad luck. I just escaped from that hateful woman. Then I met this great evildoer! I almost lost my pants! I say, when you transcend in the future, you must repay me handsomely!"

"To save you, I've really suffered heavy losses. I'm not lying this time. You must be careful next time..." The Boundless Wall babbled, sighing.

Knowing the Boundless Wall's greedy nature, yet it was willing to give up a pile of treasures from its belly to save her, Bai Shuyue couldn't help but feel a warmth in her heart.

"Don't worry, if I have the day of transcendence, I will never forget your past help." Bai Shuyue said very solemnly.

The Boundless Wall was overjoyed upon hearing this.

Little did it know that Bai Shuyue's promise was far more significant than it imagined, because Bai Shuyue was not referring to this life, but to every life since their contact and in the future.

In adversity, the relationship between the two deepened.

With the Boundless Wall leading Bai Shuyue to flee at extreme speed, they soon arrived at the fringe of the Utmost Dark Star Sea.

Seeing the continuous world remnants in the distance, Bai Shuyue was filled with shock.

"As long as we cross the formation ahead, we should be able to escape from here."

"But..."

"This formation seems to have a dual guiding path, pointing to different locations." The Boundless Wall pondered after observing for a while.

"Forget it, it's better not to attract trouble. Finding a safe place to cultivate is the most important thing." Soon, the Boundless Wall came to a conclusion.

Without consulting Bai Shuyue, it charged towards the high wall.

A green light flashed, and they crossed over.

In the next instant, Bai Shuyue had already left the Utmost Dark Star Sea.

"The Great Dao... seems to be a bit richer." Bai Shuyue sensed it keenly.

However, due to the lesson just learned, she was very cautious and did not immediately comprehend the Great Dao. Instead, she observed carefully first.

The Boundless Wall sped towards the vibrant area in the distance.

"I knew it! There must be other places suitable for cultivation outside the Profound Yellow Realm!"

Seeing that the Northern Star Sea was getting closer, the Boundless Wall became a little too conceited.

"Cultivate well, and transcend as soon as possible."

It exhorted Bai Shuyue.

Suddenly, for some unknown reason, the speed of the Boundless Wall's flight slowed down.

Innumerable crisscrossing thin lines appeared around, like a giant net, blocking the Boundless Wall.

Before the Boundless Wall and Bai Shuyue could react, they had already plunged headfirst into the Dao net.

"Immortal artifact?" With a cold snort, a transparent giant hand appeared in the void and instantly grabbed the Boundless Wall.

Having escaped from the Hungry Immortal and Ho Gan Immortal, the Boundless Wall had exhausted its foundation. At this moment, it could no longer struggle and was slowly suppressed by the Dao net.

"Damn it! How could this be!" Before the Boundless Wall's consciousness disappeared, it cried out in rage.

Inside the Boundless Wall, looking at the layered defensive formations that were about to shatter around them, Bai Shuyue sighed.

"It's too dangerous outside. It's better to just stay honestly in the Profound Yellow Realm."

Her eyes flickered, and she made up her mind.

Bai Shuyue died by suicide for the second time!

However, the reincarnation did not end here.

A green light flashed, and the Boundless Wall, which was clearly suppressed by the Dao net, instantly escaped.

It disappeared in an instant without a trace.

The transparent giant hand was full of surprise. It searched around for a long time, but ultimately could no longer find the trace of the Boundless Wall.

In the center of the Utmost Dark Star Sea, where the Star Sea Origin True Intent was most active.

The Boundless Wall suddenly appeared.

"Hahaha, I knew I wouldn't die!"

Overjoyed at regaining its freedom, the Boundless Wall was extremely proud.

When it checked its surroundings, it was suddenly alarmed: "Oh no, why did I come back here?"

Then, it let out a muffled cry: "Ah, alas, little girl, why did you die so easily! It's over, it's all over..."

Just as the Boundless Wall was unable to accept it and kept wailing.

Li Fan quietly appeared beside his aunt's corpse, looking at her face that had been stripped of its features. He gently placed his hands on it.

"Presumptuous! Who are you, to dare defile..." The Boundless Wall hadn't finished speaking before its voice abruptly stopped, as if its mouth had been gagged.

But it wasn't Li Fan who stopped him. Li Fan was examining his aunt's memories from before her death.

Instead, upon seeing Li Fan, the Boundless Wall was so terrified that it couldn't speak.

Having seen countless True Immortals in the Immortal Realm, even successive Immortal Emperors.

Its intuition told the Boundless Wall that the person before it was very dangerous.

Extremely dangerous!

The Boundless Wall's intuition was not ordinary. It was able to survive the great tribulation of the Immortal Realm's destruction and even plunder the Dao Palace, all thanks to its intuition.

And the reason it was willing to reveal its treasured possessions to Bai Shuyue was because its intuition told it that Bai Shuyue was a good person. Helping her once would bring ten times the reward in the future! However, it never expected that Bai Shuyue would die so easily.

Moreover, she encountered such an extremely dangerous individual...

"Don't see me, don't see me, don't see me..." The Boundless Wall repeatedly recited in its heart, not daring to make any sound.

It was like the days when it lay still on top of the Taiyi Palace years ago.

Li Fan naturally noticed the Boundless Wall's small movements.

However, at this moment, he no longer needed to concern himself with it. If the Saint Monarch himself were present, he would have taken it seriously.

It was merely a tile used by the Saint Monarch to build a Dao Palace in one of the countless possibilities...

It would be beneath Li Fan to even acknowledge it.

"Ho Gan Immortal."

"Virtuous Immortal."

"What does it have to do with me? What a Virtuous Immortal!"

Exiting from his aunt's memories, Li Fan gently brushed his hand, expelling the residual influence of immortal energy.

The blank face slowly recovered its original appearance.

The figure that his aunt had encountered just now was the Virtuous Immortal who had transformed the previous Hungry Immortal into a heavenly pillar to resist the Tribulation of Dao Annihilation!

"The Immortal Realm is in ruins, and the lower realms will not last long. Someone has to step forward and hold on for everyone, to resist the Dao Annihilation. Why can't it be you?!"

"Sacrifice the individual for the greater good. We survivors will remember you."

The Virtuous Immortal spoke these hypocritical words, and then casually killed the old Hungry Immortal.

What's more, after arriving in the lower realm, he unleashed a world-ending catastrophe.

"With the strength of a Virtuous Immortal, even destroying the lower realms would be as easy as waving a hand. However, he did not act personally, but wrote many True Immortal Seal Scripts and used their power..."

Li Fan frowned slightly.

When one's strength reaches the level of a Virtuous Immortal, they would certainly not do meaningless things.

"It seems it's related to his method of transcendence."

"And..."

With a thought, a statue appeared before Li Fan.

It was the Profound Sky God Statue, buried in the Profound Yellow Realm, beneath the old home of Su Bai.

When Li Fan had carelessly touched it in the past, he had lost his face due to its influence, which was exactly the same as his aunt's encounter.

Li Fan's appearance this time was also due to this reason.

"My previous guess was actually correct."

"Ho Gan Immortal is the Faceless Immortal. And he is also the Virtuous Immortal."

"But..."

Li Fan fell into confusion.

The Faceless Immortal had once saved the life of King Profound Sky, and even maintained contact with him after King Profound Sky returned to the Profound Yellow Realm.

His actions were completely unlike the arbitrary Virtuous Immortal.

Moreover, when Li Fan was a mortal, he had once utilized the immortal power of the Faceless Immortal and successfully escaped under its influence.

If the Faceless Immortal were truly the Virtuous Immortal, with the power he displayed, the Li Fan of that time would have had no chance of escape.

"To find the answer..."

Li Fan looked at the Profound Sky God Statue before him, and sent a wisp of his divine sense into it.

It seemed to have traversed a long stretch of darkness.

"Who?"

A vigilant voice came.

"An old friend of King Profound Sky." Li Fan said casually.

"A friend of Xuanyuan Hong?" The voice on the other side sounded somewhat confused.

"He doesn't know me, but I know him, and I've received his help many times." Li Fan explained. This statement was not a lie.

"I see."

After saying only this, the other side fell silent.

It seemed to be somewhat wary of the suddenly appearing Li Fan.

Li Fan didn't mind and asked, "Your state seems a little off? Do you need help?"

"You? Help?"

"If Xuanyuan Hong is the purest and most sincere person I have ever met, then you are the most evil and wicked person!" The Faceless Immortal coldly sneered.

Hearing this, Li Fan's eyes narrowed slightly.

"It seems you have the ability to discern people's character."

The Faceless Immortal remained silent.

"You say I am the most evil and wicked person, but I disagree. Not to mention the two suppressed in the Eternal Heavenly Steps of the Shore. Just the Virtuous Immortal I know, his actions are no less than mine! I really can't bear the title of most evil and wicked!"

"It seems your divine abilities have not been fully cultivated!" Li Fan smiled.

"..."

Like a stone cast into a calm lake, Li Fan could feel the emotional fluctuations transmitted from the Faceless Immortal.

"So you came to test me."

After a long time, the Faceless Immortal snorted coldly.

"The Virtuous Immortal is long gone."

"If you have any grievances, find him yourself."

"It has nothing to do with me!"

"It has nothing to do with me, what's it to me? You're still not him?" Li Fan said sternly.

"No wonder. Originally, King Profound Sky ordered many statues of you to be erected and worshipped by his followers in gratitude for saving his life. Later, he strictly ordered his subordinates to stop worshipping the Profound Sky God statue..."

"It must be that you discovered that you, your savior, were the culprit responsible for the catastrophe in the lower star sea!" Li Fan mused thoughtfully.

"I said, that's not me!" The Faceless Immortal retorted angrily.

"The Virtuous Immortal is not you, are you the Virtuous Immortal?" Li Fan countered.

"..."

"No." It seemed he hesitated slightly, and the Faceless Immortal paused for a long time before finally answering.

"It seems, friend, you've encountered a problem in your cultivation." Li Fan suddenly realized.

"Friend, you may not know this, but I am adept at the medical principles of heaven and earth. Even incurable diseases in the Mountains and Seas can be treated by me. If you have a problem, feel free to speak. Perhaps I can lend you a hand." Li Fan said very sincerely.

"Heh heh..." The Faceless Immortal certainly wouldn't believe Li Fan's nonsense.

However, for some unknown reason, he still explained: "It's not really a problem with cultivation. It's just an inevitable part of the path to transcendence."

"I was once the renowned [Virtuous Immortal] of the Immortal Realm. The Dao is formless and shapeless, it gives birth to heaven and earth, and the heart is free from defilement. Virtue is encompassing and glorious, cultivating harmony and acting without regret. Dao and Virtue are one, with the mind and nature as the root."

"What I follow is not worldly morality in the usual sense, but the principles circulating in heaven and earth. However..."

The Faceless Immortal sighed with emotion: "The erosion of Dao Annihilation and the collapse of heaven and earth. Even heaven and earth themselves can hardly circulate completely, so the [Virtue] I cultivate naturally developed various loopholes. To repair these loopholes, I began to seek solutions."

"I never expected to embark on an unexpected path."

"Consciousness split and out of control?" Li Fan asked.

"Far more than that. It's that Dao and Virtue have separated." The Faceless Immortal's words were somewhat helpless.

"Dao, Virtue, separated, family?" Li Fan savored these four words carefully.

"I am Virtue, he is Dao. We cannot have both Dao and Virtue, so we parted ways."

"He lost the constraints of Virtue, acted recklessly and without fear. He considered his own actions and thoughts as Virtue, and surprisingly achieved complete Virtue and even advanced further."

"As for me..."

"His actions became my demonic obstacle. Virtue is no longer Virtue, hence the loss of my face."

"I can't even take care of myself, how can I still seek the Dao? Compared to him, I have fallen far behind." The Faceless Immortal said with some melancholy.

Li Fan was not a dull person. With the Faceless Immortal's explanation, he quickly understood.

But recalling his aunt's experience just now, he quickly discovered a flaw in the Virtuous Immortal's possible reasoning.

He couldn't help but laugh heartily: "I think you are trapped in the situation and do not understand it clearly!"

"What do you mean?" The Faceless Immortal asked in surprise.

"Dao and Virtue. Although Dao and Virtue are separated, they are ultimately interdependent. If Virtue is no longer Virtue, then the face is lost. But if Dao loses Virtue, what then?"

"I think, in the end, you still can't escape the fate of being 'faceless'." Li Fan said with a light smile.

"If I'm not mistaken, that Virtuous Immortal should also be looking for you, right?"

The Faceless Immortal remained silent.

Li Fan's heart became even more certain.

"Dao and Virtue separated, perhaps allowing temporary transcendence."

"However, to truly achieve perfection, you two will eventually have to reunite."

Chapter 1627: Intuition Jumps Across Reincarnation

"True perfection..." The Faceless Immortal murmured, unable to hide his longing.

"What you said is indeed reasonable. But now, he is strong and I am weak. If so-called [perfection] truly requires us to merge again, I'm afraid I will be the one being hunted."

"You probably already know this in your heart, which is why you've been hiding." Li Fan's words pierced through the veil of truth.

"As for him being strong and you being weak... You don't need to worry. With my help, that mere Ho Gan Immortal is nothing to fear."

The Faceless Immortal had a clear understanding of the specific strength of his other self. As for Li Fan, they had only communicated remotely through the stone statue of the Faceless Immortal.

Therefore, he still harbored some doubt about Li Fan's confident attitude: "The Virtuous Immortal has long since transcended. What kind of strength and realm do you possess to be so confident?"

Li Fan smiled: "I am merely a minor transcended being. The reason I say he is nothing to fear is not because of me, but because of you."

"Me?" The Faceless Immortal was slightly taken aback.

"To attack the enemy's vital point. With you here, I don't have to search the entire Mountains and Seas for him. I just need to deliberately expose your whereabouts, then set a trap, and wait for the rabbit. By planning with intention while he acts without, we will naturally have the upper hand." Li Fan said indifferently.

"Why would you choose to help me? Are you enemies with him, or is it because of King Profound Sky?" The Faceless Immortal was somewhat confused inside.

"Neither, neither. I simply want to meet this Virtuous Immortal."

Li Fan's answer was somewhat unexpected to the Faceless Immortal, so he fell into a long silence.

"Although he is immoral and lawless, his own flow of virtue approaches perfection. He is extremely sensitive to all disharmonious malevolence in the outside world. Trying to scheme against him... is probably extremely difficult."

"The more information you give me about him, the higher our chances of victory."

Li Fan's words left the Faceless Immortal speechless.

Not agreeing to Li Fan immediately, the Faceless Immortal, after a period of silence, simply replied, "I need to consider it carefully."

"It's fine, I was just struck by a sudden impulse. When you've made up your mind, you can contact me again through this stone statue anytime."

"Oh, and one more thing..." Just as the aura in the stone statue was about to disappear, Li Fan suddenly asked again.

"Back when the Profound Sky Sect was destroyed and King Profound Sky mysteriously disappeared, the head of your statue in the Profound Sky Sect inexplicably cracked open. Then, the Kui Beast even sent a message towards your location..."

"Do you know what King Profound Sky encountered back then? And what message did he convey to you?" Li Fan's gaze was deep.

The response was a long silence.

"When he encountered danger back then, I did indeed help him. But the one who truly protected him was someone else. As for what he told me..."

"I cannot tell you this." After saying these words, the aura in the Profound Sky stone statue completely disappeared.

Li Fan chuckled: "As expected of the [Virtue] within the [Virtuous Immortal]."

He put away the stone statue of the Faceless Immortal at will. This life was not in a hurry to end the True Reversion. He would see if there was an opportunity to meet that Virtuous Immortal.

"Don't see me, don't see me, don't see me..." The Boundless Wall was still frantically muttering in its heart, praying for Li Fan's departure.

And as if it were meant to be, Li Fan's figure flickered and disappeared.

The Boundless Wall, which had been frozen in fear the moment before, now instantly wrapped around Bai Shuyue's corpse and fled madly towards the high wall.

"I knew I would be able to turn misfortune into fortune in the end."

"But it's really been unlucky recently, encountering dangers repeatedly. It's best to find a safe place and hide for a while."

"When crossing the Star Sea formation, there will be a dual guiding path. Since the Star Sea outside the formation is inaccessible, let's go to another place..." Having made up its mind, the Boundless Wall hurried to escape.

Just as the high wall was in sight, a figure appeared in front of them again, blocking their path.

It was Li Fan again!

The Boundless Wall's heart tightened, and its body abruptly stopped.

"Why did this ominous god come back again?"

"Don't see me, don't see me..."

Having no other way to cope, the Boundless Wall could only brace itself and recite in its heart once more.

However, this time, the incantation was ineffective.

"I'm a little curious why you're so nice to her?"

"You don't seem like the type to give without expecting anything in return. Although her talent is indeed extraordinary, and she has hope of transcending. But that's something that will happen years later. For a hypothetical future, you're even willing to deplete all your accumulated treasures..."

"Could it be that in this short time, you've developed genuine feelings for her?"

Li Fan flicked his finger slightly, and the Boundless Wall fell into his palm.

The "she" in his words naturally referred to Bai Shuyue.

Although the Boundless Wall's own value was no longer considered by Li Fan.

But after seeing the Boundless Wall, upon escaping, still wanting to take his aunt's corpse with it, Li Fan became interested in the Boundless Wall's motivations.

He had long seen through the Boundless Wall's personality.

Under normal circumstances, it would never have put in such a great effort for Bai Shuyue, who was still a mortal. But it did so...

When something unusual happens, there must be a reason.

Although he was asking, Li Fan did not expect the Boundless Wall to answer honestly.

His fingers tightened slightly, and wisps of faint light penetrated into the Boundless Wall.

All of the Boundless Wall's thoughts turned into flowing light, which were then mastered and absorbed by Li Fan.

"Helping her a little will bring ten times the reward in the future. This kind of intuition..." Reading the Boundless Wall's motives, Li Fan's eyebrows couldn't help but lightly raise.

As the manipulator behind the scenes, Li Fan naturally knew that the Boundless Wall's intuition was extremely accurate.

Li Fan, whose intuition for warning was also incredibly accurate, naturally became interested in this kind of "intuition."

"One part of effort, ten parts of reward. It's both accurate and inaccurate. Currently, my aunt's talent is not enough to reach transcendence in this life. Therefore, the probability of her bringing sufficient rewards to the Boundless Wall in this life tends towards zero."

"But the Boundless Wall's intuition is so strong."

"Could it be that its direct foreseen reward is not directed at this life, but rather..."

"Dozens of lives after continuous True Reversion?"

Li Fan's eyes narrowed slightly.

He couldn't help but gently stroke the surface of the Boundless Wall with his hands.

At first glance, this guess might seem absurd.

What right did a mere tile used to build a Dao Palace have to foresee the future after True Reversion?

But...

The reason why Li Fan could think of this wildly was also guided by his own intuition!

Li Fan trusted his intuition implicitly.

"Infinite Spirituality is the fundamental reason for True Reversion attaching itself to me."

"And my intuition is also extraordinary, but I have never found a reasonable explanation."

"This Boundless Wall might be the key to a breakthrough."

"Intuitive warning, to this extent?" Li Fan tapped the Boundless Wall with his fingers continuously, pondering in his heart.

Such spiritual sense seemed extremely rare in the Mountains and Seas.

But perhaps it was just normally imperceptible.

"With the infinity of the Mountains and Seas, it is common for creatures to have moments of inspiration and avoid misfortune by instinct at critical junctures."

"Avoiding misfortune, avoiding misfortune..."

Li Fan suddenly thought of Yin Yuzhen, the shopkeeper of the Heavenly Treasure Pavilion on Glazed Island, whom he had met when he first started cultivating.

Although she was a mortal, she possessed the ability to avoid misfortune and seek good fortune.

It was precisely because she foresaw the infinite benefits of helping Li Fan in the future that she repeatedly gave generously without reservation.

"A mortal body naturally cannot perceive that I possess True Reversion."

"But it can point to my future."

"..."

Li Fan then thought about how, later, when he established the Bright Moon Palace on Bright Moon Lake and lent money extensively to collect contribution points, which eventually attracted the attention of Dao Integration cultivators for cleanup.

Yin Yuzhen had also predicted great fortune at that time.

Later, the Dao Integration cultivators sensed Lan Yu's Devouring Origin Samsara Array and left at the critical moment, sparing everyone's lives.

At that time, Li Fan also thought that this great fortune corresponded to this event.

But...

"Although we narrowly escaped death, we still went through great danger, which can hardly be called great fortune."

"The true great fortune refers to..."

Relying solely on his own thoughts to deduce, he could hardly find an answer.

But if he followed his intuition and reasoned accordingly, Li Fan once again arrived at an unimaginable conclusion.

"This great fortune also points to me!"

"Before this, the Yin sisters were always under my control, being exploited by me. They worked tirelessly every day managing the Bright Moon Palace affairs, and although their cultivation talent gradually improved, they ultimately could not advance further."

"But after this near-death escape..."

"I encountered that Nascent Soul cultivator. Although I defeated the Nascent Soul cultivator with the cultivation of Foundation Establishment, it ultimately ended in mutual destruction, ending that life."

"My death was a great fortune for Yin Yuzhen."

"This is only natural. Just like a bird leaving its cage, or a tiger returning to the mountains. From then on, the sisters were no longer oppressed and enslaved by me."

"But..."

Li Fan was slightly stunned: "After I perform True Reversion, everything in the Mountains and Seas is reset. Everything returns to its starting point."

"Where does the great fortune come from?"

"Am I overthinking it, or..."

As if touching upon the secret of True Reversion, Li Fan's heart involuntarily beat violently.

He re-organized his thoughts from the beginning. Not relying on ordinary logical reasoning, but more on his keen inner intuition. Li Fan arrived at another incredible conclusion again.

When he performed True Reversion, the original possibilities seemed to still exist and continue to develop in the direction of the river of time.

But this deduction was completely contradictory to Li Fan's own experience.

"Why?"

"Does True Reversion reset the Mountains and Seas or not?"

Before this, Li Fan had no doubt about this point.

After all, powerful beings of various possibilities, roaming between the Mountains and Seas, all returned to their initial anchor points with his "True Reversion."

But when his own intuition pointed to a completely opposite conclusion.

Li Fan was somewhat bewildered.

Based on his current understanding of "intuition," he could not explain such a contradiction at all.

"Fortunately, I have discovered the trick."

"It's a pity that the Profound Yellow Realm has already been destroyed in this life, otherwise, I could have captured Yin Yuzhen and studied her carefully."

"This Boundless Wall is also a subject worth paying attention to."

In Li Fan's past reincarnations, there were not many beings with such extremely sharp intuition as him.

The Boundless Wall's own attributes were mediocre, but this intuition was perhaps rare in the Mountains and Seas.

"Do Yin Yuzhen and the Boundless Wall in other possibilities also have this intuition?"

"Or..."

Li Fan's gaze suddenly sharpened.

He had traveled through one percent of the Mountains and Seas, and various possibilities of the Profound Yellow Realm existed in different circumstances.

"Perhaps, in these Profound Yellow Realms, there are no other Yin Yuzhens."

"Just like me."

"The Li Fans in other possibilities are not Li Fan."

"Intuition originates from [Uniqueness of the Mountains and Seas]?" Li Fan's heart stirred.

"The current Mountains and Seas are not complete Mountains and Seas, not the true [Infinity], but a remnant eroded by Dao Annihilation, riddled with holes."

"Theoretically, there are indeed some beings that are unique to the Mountains and Seas."

"This Uniqueness of the Mountains and Seas is similar to the perfect transcendence realm of unifying oneself, but fundamentally different."

"Transcendence is complete because of the Uniqueness of the Mountains and Seas."

"And these special beings are weak because of the Uniqueness of the Mountains and Seas."

"Of course, I am not among them."

"These beings, although destined to have no chance of transcending, have awakened extremely special divine abilities as a blessing in disguise. Perhaps, every perfected transcended expert possesses this ability?" Li Fan did not know.

The most crucial point was that this peculiar intuition sensed the abnormality in the True Reversion cycle.

"It seems that the mechanism of True Reversion is not as simple as I imagined."

"What is true becomes false, and what is false becomes true..."

Li Fan repeated these seven characters internally, concealing his figure, and released his finger from the Boundless Wall.

As if waking from a deep sleep, the Boundless Wall remained stunned for a long time before finally regaining consciousness.

"I'm actually fine? That villain actually let me go?"

The Boundless Wall was stunned for a moment, then overjoyed: "As expected, good fortune favors the lucky!"

Looking around, it found that Bai Shuyue's corpse was still there.

After a pause, its heart became entangled.

Ultimately, it followed its instincts, wrapped Bai Shuyue's corpse, and continued to flee madly towards the high wall.

"There should still be a chance to save her."

Soon, the Boundless Wall, carrying Bai Shuyue, crossed the high wall again.

This time, a radiant green light surged from the Boundless Wall.

Revealing its true form as an immortal artifact, it traversed and arrived at a ruined Ke Dao field!

"Where is this?"

The Boundless Wall scanned its surroundings, somewhat surprised and uncertain.

"It looks somewhat familiar..."

"Fortunately, there seems to be no threat."

Muttering to itself, the Boundless Wall delved deeper into the Ke Dao field.

It arrived at the control center of the Dao field.

Looking at the various objects scattered on the ground, the Boundless Wall identified them carefully and couldn't help but be delighted: "The facilities are actually still in good condition."

A deep green light swept across the surrounding space.

The originally static inanimate objects seemed to come alive,

Presenting phantom images of their past.

"So this is how it's done, and so it's done."

The Boundless Wall recorded the manipulation methods for each object, feeling extremely smug.

"I was chosen by the Saint Monarch, how could I not have some skills!"

Chapter 1628: Mountains and Seas Die and Revive

As the Boundless Wall continued to manipulate, a miraculous sight suddenly appeared on the cylindrical hall walls of the Ke Dao field.

Countless diced blocks stood side by side, coexisting in dark, dim, and flashing states.

In just a moment, the Boundless Wall understood the meaning represented by these different blocks.

"My premonition was correct, there are still so many thriving star seas! I can just hide in any one of them and wait for the wind to pass."

The Boundless Wall's green light swept across the cylindrical hall walls, selecting a suitable target.

Naturally, it also noticed the conspicuous large white circles.

"Drawing a circle to create a prison. It should be for selection and screening. This Dao field was inexplicably abandoned. Could it be that they have already found their target?"

"Regardless, the star seas within the white circles are superior in all conditions to those outside. We should prioritize them."

After careful selection, the Boundless Wall finally chose a peculiar world of Mountains and Seas!

A vast blue ocean, stretching endlessly. The seawater overflowed, occupying the entire star field. And its source was actually a mountain peak no bigger than a palm.

Spring water flowed down from the summit, continuously flowing day and night, turning into a star sea!

"Although this Mountains and Seas is not *that* Mountains and Seas, its shape, image, and essence coincide with the Mountains and Seas, so it must have its unspeakable profoundness."

"Moreover, this spring of life on the mountain peak might be able to bring the young lady back to life!"

The Boundless Wall transformed into a streak of green light and entered it.

It arrived directly at the summit.

From the outside, the mountain in the Star Sea world appeared to be merely a rather low peak. However, upon entering the realm, one discovered that this mountain was actually over ten million feet tall.

It was just that apart from the vast blue ocean covering the star field, there were no other reference points, which made the mountain appear short.

The mountain was not short, and the water was naturally not simple stream water.

It was like a great river or ocean, rumbling and roaring, emitting a deafening sound like thunder.

It wasn't just a simple sound, but it was accompanied by the rhythm of this star field's Great Dao.

Even the Boundless Wall, as it passed through, was inevitably affected. The sound and water rhythms were like carving knives, trying to etch marks on the surface of the Boundless Wall.

A faint light flashed, and the Boundless Wall blocked all anomalies, continuing to fly towards the summit.

"Strange, I was clearly flying directly towards the summit, but I landed halfway up..."

"Could it be that I've been damaged too severely, and can't even grasp this bit of precision?"

The Boundless Wall, climbing slowly, fell into a brief period of self-doubt.

However, after flying towards the summit for a while longer, it finally realized: "This mountain peak is actually inhabited?!"

The Boundless Wall's flight abruptly halted. Amidst the roar of the cascading spring water, it hesitated for a long time. Finally, the Boundless Wall gritted its teeth and decided to continue.

"In front of the Saint Monarch, I borrow this treasure land. I believe the owner of this place will not blame me."

The Boundless Wall's vision was extraordinary, and it could naturally discern the sophistication of the formation that had silently repelled it, capable of astonishing the summit.

"Although not transcended, at least it's a strong individual with no name. What a hell, even when the Immortal Realm still existed, there weren't many experts of this level. How is it that after the destruction of the Immortal Realm, this corner of the lower realm is full of them!" The Boundless Wall felt depressed in its heart.

"However... there should be no great risk."

While flying and observing, the Boundless Wall finally reached a conclusion.

Its intuition was indeed very accurate.

For nine consecutive times, the Boundless Wall was repelled, but it persisted relentlessly and continued to ascend.

On the tenth attempt, the formation finally ceased to activate.

The Boundless Wall was thus able to bring Bai Shuyue to the summit.

"Such vigorous life force..." The Boundless Wall looked at a small spring eye ahead, which was the source of the ocean covering this world, and was awed by the vast life force contained within.

At the same time, it did not forget to throw Bai Shuyue's corpse onto it.

The corpse blocked the spring eye. The surging water flow gradually weakened, and the roaring sound of the water subsided.

When the Star Sea world that formed its own cycle finally fell into silence, no more spring water flowed from the mountain peak.

The cycle of Mountains and Seas that was self-contained was broken. The vast life force that was originally concentrated in the Mountains and Seas now slowly focused on Bai Shuyue's corpse.

A mere mortal corpse should not be able to suppress the spring eye of the Mountains and Seas world.

Seeing Bai Shuyue's corpse, which could be washed away by the spring water at any moment, the Boundless Wall revealed its original form.

It gently pressed down on it.

A divine stone fell, and the Mountains and Seas were suppressed.

With Bai Shuyue's corpse as the core of the cycle, this Star Sea world entered a new balance.

"Sigh, I hope this works."

The Boundless Wall felt the changes in Bai Shuyue's body under the impact of the vast life force.

"Hmm, it's good that I came in time, she wasn't completely dead yet."

"In a hundred years, she should be able to come back to life."

"It's a pity that I, this great master, can't get half the benefits from this spring of life. Little lady, when you become successful in the future, don't forget me!" The Boundless Wall kept muttering.

The Boundless Wall did not notice that the moment it and Bai Shuyue occupied the spring eye at the summit, a divine sense had already noticed them.

Naturally, it was the owner of this Mountains and Seas world, the disciple appointed by Duke Shou Qiu, Cheng Dao.

However, Cheng Dao had already left the Star Sea and was outside the high wall. He had no intention of occupying the sacred land that had nurtured his consciousness. Since another fated person had found it, and it was for saving a life, Cheng Dao did not drive them away.

Of course, if it were ordinary mortal objects, Cheng Dao would definitely not be so polite.

The most crucial point was that Cheng Dao sensed the aura from the Immortal Realm's Saint Monarch on the Boundless Wall.

Cheng Dao was a disciple of Duke Shou Qiu. The other party was from before the Saint Monarch's gate.

Naturally, he would give some face.

The phrase "before the Saint Monarch's gate" was sometimes worthless, and sometimes worth a thousand pieces of gold!

In addition to considering the other party's origin, as the one guarding the back of the Star Sea Dao Net, Cheng Dao also discovered that the Boundless Wall currently appearing in the Mountains and Seas was an immortal artifact that had suddenly appeared in the Dao Net not long ago and then suddenly disappeared after being captured.

According to the residual fluctuations of the Dao Net at that time, Cheng Dao found that the person behind the scenes who helped the Boundless Wall escape at that time was actually not weaker than himself.

Since they possessed such strength, it was natural to resolve enmity rather than forge it.

Cheng Dao had always been principled.

...

Bai Shuyue lay quietly in the Star Sea world for more than fifty years, and her life force gradually recovered.

However, the Boundless Wall, which had witnessed all the changes from life and death, discovered something strange.

"Life force has slightly recovered, and consciousness has been reborn from dissipation."

"But..."

"After experiencing this life and death tribulation, her ignorant consciousness has not shown the slightest fear?"

The Boundless Wall gasped.

One must know that to have experienced life and death and yet not fear it is truly rare in ancient times.

"Coupled with her almost heaven-defying talent..."

"My premonition was indeed correct!"

"Transcendence is within reach!"

Chapter 1629: He Ganxian's Cultivation

Just as Bai Shuyue was gradually coming back to life, Li Fan finally received the reply from the Faceless Immortal.

"The Virtuous Immortal is planning something extremely important."

"If this matter can be prevented, it might be possible to somewhat reverse the power gap between him and me."

The "him" referred to in the words was naturally the "Dao" part of the Virtuous Immortal, Ho Gan Immortal.

The Faceless Immortal was truly cautious. After more than fifty years of consideration, he still had no intention of revealing himself. Instead, he waited for a good opportunity, to test Li Fan's "sincerity" without revealing himself.

Li Fan's original goal was to meet Ho Gan Immortal, so he didn't care and directly asked about this so-called "good opportunity."

"The Virtuous Immortal... Ho Gan Immortal acts according to his whims. He has no virtue, so everything he thinks and does is attributed to virtue." Perhaps because he felt that the name Li Fan had given to his other self was more fitting, the Faceless Immortal simply changed his address.

"Although to others, and even to me, Ho Gan Immortal's actions seem shocking and go against conscience, in his own eyes, what he does is incomparably correct and unquestionable."

"Ho Gan Immortal's understanding of this concept is supported by a self-consistent logic. Note that Ho Gan Immortal does not think he is truly without virtue. He actually has virtue, but this virtue is contrary to the common understanding and only exists within his own cognition."

"For so many years, no outsider has broken this cognition. He acts according to what he believes is correct, and his flow of virtue becomes stronger, continuing to practice his own ideals. For so many years, Ho Gan Immortal has been repeating this cycle, growing stronger like a snowball."

"But now, there is the best opportunity to subvert his cognition!"

The Faceless Immortal spoke eloquently.

Sensing the faint fluctuations coming from the Profound Sky stone statue, Li Fan pondered, interrupted the Faceless Immortal, and said: "You can actually perceive Ho Gan Immortal's specific movements? It seems I underestimated you before."

"Ho Gan Immortal is not without virtue, and the Faceless Immortal is not without Dao. You two are quite interesting."

"I said earlier that Dao and Virtue separating is not as simple as splitting into two. The intricacies can only be known by ourselves. It is truly not worth mentioning to outsiders." The Faceless Immortal gave a perfunctory explanation and then detailed the great opportunity to intercept Ho Gan Immortal.

It actually touched upon the most fundamental core of Ho Gan Immortal's own moral concept.

"The reason Ho Gan Immortal believes himself to have virtue is that in his eyes, he is the only one who can save the Mountains and Seas. Therefore, all other beings in the Mountains and Seas must yield to his survival, even if it means sacrificing them. It is only right and proper."

"Therefore, he can righteously destroy an entire possibility just for a minuscule improvement in his own strength."

Hearing the Faceless Immortal's words, Li Fan's heart stirred, and a thought flashed through his mind. His eyes narrowed slightly.

However, he did not show it on the surface, merely letting the Faceless Immortal continue.

"The foundation of all his actions is built upon his ultimate salvation of the Mountains and Seas. If this fantasy can be shattered, it will sever his self-circulation of virtue. Even without us taking action, as time passes, his strength will only continue to decline."

"Currently, he is creating boundless slaughter in another possibility within the Mountains and Seas, just to increase his strength again. If he is defeated at this time, causing him to lose his inexplicable confidence, he will be utterly demoralized!"

Li Fan keenly sensed the key point in the Faceless Immortal's words: "At this time... meaning, even if he is defeated normally, it won't shake his self-concept. It must be at a critical moment. So, is he comprehending the Dao now?"

"That's right! Rather than comprehending the Dao, it's more accurate to say he's [cultivating]. Ho Gan Immortal's cultivation method is different from ordinary people. Although he has found an excuse for slaughtering all living beings, saying it's all for the ultimate salvation of the Mountains and Seas, in his heart, he still retains some correct conscience. He knows that what he's doing goes against human ethics. He needs to diminish the influence of his remaining correct conscience on himself while his strength grows."

"Specifically, he deliberately creates a karmic debt of slaughter. When his remaining conscience feels the karma he has created, he will feel guilt and sorrow, and may even enter a state of temporary loss of control."

"But after Ho Gan Immortal suppresses these negative emotions, he can regain his sanity. And the remaining conscience will be consumed. When all conscience is gone, he will have no more flaws. And now, after these years of cultivation, he has very little conscience left."

"Every cultivation is the best opportunity to stop him. I've tried to intervene before, but unfortunately, the power gap was too great..."

"You might as well go and investigate this possibility."

The Faceless Immortal sent a coordinate for a location in the Mountains and Seas, and then his aura completely disappeared.

Perhaps it was to avoid something, he was extremely cautious.

"These two..."

"One Dao, one Virtue."

After thinking for a moment, Li Fan followed the Faceless Immortal's guidance, leaped up, and left the Primordial Possibility.

Heading towards the Mountains and Seas to find his whereabouts.

It was quite far from the Primordial.

Fortunately, in the Mountains and Seas, there was no concept of time passage.

When Li Fan arrived at the destination possibility, he saw a scene of doomsday.

Ho Gan Immortal stood in the center of the shattered star sea, laughing maniacally: "What does it have to do with me, what does it have to do with me!"

He was indifferent to the countless beings perishing around him.

Li Fan could vaguely see that while saying these words, his face indeed showed signs of grief.

It appeared extremely contradictory.

But his body remained unmoving.

Watching the star sea, and even the possibility, being destroyed.

Tears gradually dried, and his expression slowly became resolute.

Even somewhat sinister.

All beings were fleeing and crying out in despair.

Li Fan, who suddenly appeared and was extremely calm at this moment, was extremely conspicuous.

Instantly, Ho Gan Immortal's gaze locked onto Li Fan.

Without any wasted words, his cold gaze directly locked onto Li Fan: "Die!"

In Li Fan's ears, it seemed to be the sound of a wooden door closing, "creak."

It was not an auditory hallucination, but the sound of the collapse of the Heavenly Emperor caused by too many pillars of the Great Dao collapsing simultaneously!

Along with the destruction of the possibility, came the Dao Annihilation tribulation like waves.

The possibility, which was already in a state of doomsday, was completely destroyed by Li Fan's appearance.

Li Fan, naturally, would not feel any self-reproach for this.

Instead, he let out a soft exclamation: "Well done!"

Chapter 1630: Birth of a Saint

Li Fan not only had no intention of scolding him but even looked excited.

"Traversing the Mountains and Seas, I finally found someone who shares my ideals today!"

Saying this, Li Fan casually unleashed a flame.

The raging fire instantly ignited the remnants of the possibility.

Ho Gan Immortal, who was originally hostile, was stunned.

"If I wish to overturn the Mountains and Seas, fellow Daoist, would you join me?"

Li Fan asked with extreme sincerity.

The last remnants of possibility were completely burned away in the True Spirit's fire.

The waves of Dao Annihilation surged towards them.

Above Li Fan's head, a solitary sail was suspended, resisting the Devouring of Dao Annihilation.

As for Ho Gan Immortal's situation, it was quite strange.

It was as if an invisible vortex existed around him, and the Dao Annihilation that flowed and eroded, after a period of circulation, was actually directed towards other areas of the Mountains and Seas.

Naturally, innocent possibilities suffered as a result, but Ho Gan Immortal, who had already left the state of Dao Comprehension, no longer felt any guilt in his heart.

In the turbulent Dao Annihilation, Li Fan and Ho Gan Immortal faced each other.

"Overturn the Mountains and Seas?" Ho Gan Immortal carefully savored these four words.

"The great tide of Dao Annihilation surges mightily. Follow it and prosper, defy it and perish! The Shore and the Saint Dynasty are but like striking a stone with an egg. Hopeless struggle only brings ridicule!"

"Why not follow the general trend, destroy the Mountains and Seas, and return to the Divine Stars. When order is rebuilt in the future, perhaps you and I can be canonized for our merits!"

"Fellow Daoist, what do you think?" Li Fan said with extreme fervor.

The Mountains and Seas seemed to fall into deathly silence.

Only the surging waves of Dao Annihilation silently devoured the innocent and unsuspecting possibilities.

"What kind of trash..."

Silently gazing at Li Fan, Ho Gan Immortal actually uttered such an evaluation after a while.

"Die!"

As if too lazy to waste words with Li Fan, Ho Gan Immortal reached out towards Li Fan from afar and grabbed.

Not only were the surrounding Dao Annihilation and Li Fan's unleashed True Spirit Fire affected, forming shackles like a vortex of confinement, but Li Fan's heart also immediately flashed with his past actions.

Destroying the Profound Yellow Realm, destroying the Utmost Dark Star Sea, destroying the high walls, destroying the Northern Star Sea, destroying the Primordial, igniting the Mountains and Seas...

The scale of the calamities created gradually expanded.

The number of beings who died in the calamities also increased exponentially.

What was originally just a string of numbers now transformed into specific faces, roaring in anger within Li Fan's heart.

As if vengeful spirits were coming to claim their lives!

Facing these countless vengeful spirits he had caused the deaths of, if Li Fan harbored the slightest guilt, he would have been affected, his mind shaken, or even lost control, and finally swallowed by these evolved vengeful spirits.

But...

Li Fan watched these suddenly appearing countless vengeful spirits with extreme indifference from beginning to end. His heart was completely motionless.

He even had a hint of doubt in his eyes.

As if these deceased vengeful spirits had nothing to do with him!

If Ho Gan Immortal's "What does it have to do with me?" was a fierce conflict between his remaining conscience and his self, then Li Fan's "What does it have to do with me?" was a simple, emotionless rhetorical question.

Ho Gan Immortal still had remnants of conscience.

Then Li Fan was...

Facing Li Fan, who was even more like "Ho Gan Immortal" than himself, Ho Gan Immortal inexplicably felt a sense of fear and instinctively wanted to escape.

However, before him, strands of True Spirit Fire suddenly erupted, blocking his path.

"Where are you going, fellow Daoist? Could it be that you believe the Mountains and Seas can still be saved?" Li Fan questioned sternly.

His previous actions were precisely for this sentence.

His voice was like thunder, striking Ho Gan Immortal's heart. Ho Gan Immortal's body trembled violently.

"Can the Mountains and Seas be saved?"

Li Fan pressed relentlessly, continuing to question.

The True Spirit Fire and the waves of Dao Annihilation complemented each other.

As water drowned and fire burned, a terrifying wave of annihilation spread through the Mountains and Seas. Even though, at first, it only affected a negligible amount of possibility.

But with Li Fan's insistent questioning, this terrifying catastrophe spread at an unimaginable speed.

Watching as it became unstoppable!

Trapped between the True Spirit Fire and the Dao Annihilation, having tried several times to escape but finding no way out, and seeing the spreading catastrophe covering the land, the increasingly pressured Ho Gan Immortal finally couldn't hold on any longer.

He roared in a choked voice, "Of course the Mountains and Seas can be saved! The one who can save the Mountains and Seas is only me, the Virtuous Immortal!"

Finally, Li Fan had forced out the deepest thought in his heart.

Li Fan, however, relentlessly continued to question, "Virtuous Immortal? Who exactly are you?"

"Do you have Dao? Do you have Virtue?"

Ho Gan Immortal refused to answer, staring at the Mountains and Seas gradually being engulfed by water and fire, his heart growing increasingly anxious.

He rushed left and right, trying to break through Li Fan's blockade.

However, his strength was already inferior to Li Fan's. The True Spirit Fire and the waves of Dao Annihilation were powers that could even devour the Mountains and Seas.

Li Fan was not confronting him head-on, but merely using the power of burning mountains and annihilating seas to surround him.

Ho Gan Immortal, with all his might, was for a time helpless, like a trapped beast in a cage, letting out sounds of pain.

"Do you have Dao? Do you have Virtue?"

Li Fan's questioning never stopped.

Each sound was like a sharp sword, piercing into Ho Gan Immortal's heart.

Ho Gan Immortal's eyes turned red, and even blood tears flowed out: "I saved the Mountains and Seas, how can I be without Virtue? I saved the Mountains and Seas, how can I be without Dao?"

The first "I saved the Mountains and Seas" was a thought, an idea.

The latter "I saved the Mountains and Seas" was concrete action and measures.

Hearing Ho Gan Immortal finally roar out this sentence, Li Fan nodded with satisfaction.

Then, his eyes revealed a cold glint, and with a wave of his hand, the True Spirit Fire burned even more vigorously.

More possibilities were affected by the flames, which surged towards the sky, completely obscuring Ho Gan Immortal's vision.

He could no longer see the Mountains and Seas, only endless flames.

From Ho Gan Immortal's perspective, it was equivalent to Li Fan burning the Mountains and Seas before his eyes.

Not only that, Li Fan then sternly questioned, "The Mountains and Seas were burned to ashes before your eyes. Did you save the Mountains and Seas?"

"How can you have Dao?"

With a final blow, Ho Gan Immortal was struck by lightning, his body stiffening andmotionless.

"Do I have Dao? Do I have Dao? Do I have Dao..."

He kept muttering to himself.

Finally, the firelight flickering in his pupils gave him the answer.

"The Mountains and Seas are burned to ashes, I have no Dao."

"I have Virtue, but no Dao."

"I have Virtue, but no Dao..."

The moment he said this, Ho Gan Immortal's body instantly became translucent.

And behind Li Fan, another figure quietly appeared.

However, Li Fan seemed to have expected his appearance!

"Because he is devoid of virtue, he lost his face."

"Your lack of virtue is not due to being affected by Ho Gan Immortal. It is because you are truly devoid of virtue!"

"Ho Gan Immortal appears to be without virtue, but in reality, he has virtue. Because all sins are committed for the sake of saving the Mountains and Seas. If the Mountains and Seas are saved, all sins will be erased."

"Virtuous Immortal, Virtuous Immortal. Ho Gan Immortal is Virtue, and you are..."

"Dao!"

With rhythmic claps, it came from the figure behind Li Fan.

It was the Faceless Immortal!

"A brilliant deduction!"

"I didn't expect you to be able to see our true nature. I thought I could use your strength to temporarily hinder his progress, but I never imagined you would do better than I expected."

"You were even able to directly categorize him."

"Truly amazing!"

The Faceless Immortal licked his lips with enjoyment as he looked at the incorporeal Ho Gan Immortal.

Ho Gan Immortal, who was not the slightest bit resistant, was absorbed into the Faceless Immortal's body.

After a moment of stillness, the aura emanating from the Faceless Immortal swelled exponentially!

As if a mortal ascended to immortality in one day!

The Faceless Immortal, who was originally extremely weak and only at the True Immortal level, now seemed to have not only inherited Ho Gan Immortal's realm and strength but also achieved some kind of qualitative transformation in his cultivation.

From the realm of Transcension, he took a step towards the path of becoming a Saint!

With the two united, the figure of Ho Gan Immortal, or rather, the [Virtuous Immortal], became more solidified.

His voice also changed. At first, it was a superposition of two voices, and then it gradually merged completely, no longer distinguishing between them.

"It has been 100,000 years in the Immortal Realm, and today I am finally complete."

"Thank you, fellow Daoist, for your help!"

The Virtuous Immortal, having completed his path to enlightenment, actually bowed to Li Fan!

"However, there is one point where you are mistaken."

"I am not Dao, and he is not Virtue."

"Dao and Virtue separated, yet Dao and Virtue are not separated."

"He is also like this."

The Virtuous Immortal's originally ethereal face slowly grew features.

He looked at Li Fan and smiled, saying: "Before we were finally [qualified], I was Dao, and he was Virtue. He was Virtue, and I was Dao."

"It's just that we chose different paths of evolution."

"I abandoned Virtue, he stuck to Virtue."

"However, my chosen Dao did not bring me the power I expected."

"And the Virtue he upheld, of saving the Mountains and Seas, and acting unscrupulously, has allowed his strength to advance by leaps and bounds."

"Originally, I was at a disadvantage and was about to fail in this race."

"Unexpectedly, unexpectedly..."

The Virtuous Immortal said with great emotion.

"Virtuous Immortal, Virtuous Immortal. Dao gives birth to Virtue, and Virtue transforms into Dao. Dao and Virtue cannot be separated!"

"This is the foundation of our existence. Even if we evolve in different directions, this point has not disappeared. If one completely loses Dao, or completely loses Virtue..."

"The basis of its existence will disappear. The consequence..."

The Virtuous Immortal's smile instantly vanished: "Is to return to one's original self."

Facing the seemingly unexpected situation, Li Fan was not alarmed.

Instead, he looked at the newly enlightened Virtuous Immortal with great interest: "So, now, do you have more Dao or more Virtue? Did you achieve enlightenment through Virtue, or through Dao?"

The Virtuous Immortal shook his head: "Whether it is Dao or Virtue is not important. What is important is who can truly reach the end."

"The victor is the Saint."

A vast aura surged from the Virtuous Immortal's body.

Like a surging wind, it instantly repelled the raging True Spirit Fire and the waves of Dao Annihilation.

Even while conversing, the Virtuous Immortal's aura had been continuously expanding.

Now, as if breaking through some kind of shackle, the intensity of his aura's expansion increased hundreds of times over.

The flames and Dao Annihilation that could devour the Mountains and Seas were suppressed by this aura of becoming a Saint.

A world-shattering radiance surged from a corner of the possibility, shooting up into the sky.

It shook the Mountains and Seas!

"Although you helped me achieve enlightenment. However..."

The [Virtue], who had just attained Sainthood, looked at Li Fan, but a strange expression suddenly appeared in his eyes.

Without any warning, his left palm pressed down like the sky collapsing.

Without even finishing a sentence, he offered Li Fan no time to react!

At the same time, it seemed as if sensing the birth of a new Saint, the Shore, the Saint Dynasty, and other areas of the Mountains and Seas,

Simultaneously cast their attention.

[Virtue] completely ignored them, his target solely being Li Fan.

"So..."

"You Saints are really annoying."

A hazy water curtain-like mist enveloped Li Fan.

As if in a state between reality and illusion, the palm of Virtue that descended from the sky passed through Li Fan's body as if it were empty space.

It was unable to cause any harm!

Virtue seemed to have anticipated this, his eyes showing a hint of surprise as he stared at the mist on Li Fan's body.

He opened his mouth, as if about to say something.

But in the end, he did not speak.
