Although I do not think highly of Wuji, in the eyes of the Master, his status might indeed be more important. If I dare to act against him, and he discovers it, I fear I will be punished." the Boundless Wall muttered.

"Secondly... too much of a good thing spoils it. Wuji's Great Dao alone is enough for us to digest for a while. With the illusory projection of Taiyi as well, it would only distract us."

Bai Shuyue eyed the Boundless Wall suspiciously, "Are you by any chance possessed by Wuji? These words do not sound like something you would say. Too much of a good thing spoils it?"

"Hmph. Do not think that I hid so many treasures before just because I wanted to devour them all. Of course, that is partly the reason. But the most important thing is to keep them in reserve. Perhaps they can save my life at a critical moment."

"If I can find better ones, I will replace them. If not, it is not impossible to devour them slowly."

The Boundless Wall shook its head and its beard.

Recalling the methods the Boundless Wall used to save her from the faceless figure in her previous life, Bai Shuyue nodded slightly.

"You are about to digest and refine Wuji, and you are still afraid of the Saint Monarch's punishment? Perhaps you can hide for a while, but you will have to face him eventually, right?" Bai Shuyue asked again.

"Who said I am going to truly devour Wuji?"

"I do not want to become the new Wuji. The fundamental purpose is not to help you break through your cultivation bottleneck?" the Boundless Wall retorted reproachfully.

Bai Shuyue was stunned.

"It is only to temporarily confine him within my belly for a period of time and borrow his strength. When you transcend, I will release him. And with his temperament, he will certainly not speak of being captured by us..." the Boundless Wall said smugly.

"Try to hold me tightly."

The Boundless Wall suddenly said to Bai Shuyue.

Bai Shuyue reached out and gently stroked the Boundless Wall, whose color had become somewhat strange.

A sense of chaotic emptiness arose in her heart.

"No wonder he was so arrogant... Is this [Wuji]?"

It was not a matter of whose Dao was strong or weak, but rather a suppression of seniority, like an elder looking at a junior.

Holding the Boundless Wall infused with Wuji, Bai Shuyue scanned the Xuanhuang world.

She felt that all the constraints in the world, the crisscrossing net of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth,

Were no longer as indestructible as before.

It was as if with a mere extension of her hand, she could tear them all apart!

"Perhaps we don't need to find another safe place to cultivate."

"We can disregard all the rules within the Xuanhuang Realm itself," Bai Shuyue suddenly spoke.

"This is the miraculous use of Wuji! How about it, am I not right! This is only the initial stage. Later, when Wuji compromises further, we can borrow even more strength."

"So, stop researching this 'new method'. Just cultivate diligently and break through as soon as possible."

Hearing this, Bai Shuyue showed a rare hint of hesitation.

Chapter 1637: The Path of Immortal Execution Ascends

Despite experiencing so much, Bai Shuyue had not forgotten her dear nephew.

The reason she had the idea of creating a new method earlier was partly for Li Fan's cultivation.

Moreover, Bai Shuyue vaguely had a premonition that the potential and prospects of the new method would surpass ordinary cultivation methods.

"The Boundless Wall's perspective is limited to this lifetime."

"And my ambition is for dozens, even hundreds of lifetimes to come! I cannot scheme against Wuji before each reincarnation. Not only is it troublesome, but there is also the possibility of failure, wasting a reincarnation opportunity."

"The new method..."

"Cannot be abandoned!"

"However, this does not conflict with my temporary cultivation of the old method to quickly improve my realm. With higher strength, my perspective will naturally change, and deducing the new method will be more efficient."

In an instant, various thoughts flashed through Bai Shuyue's mind, and she nodded to the Boundless Wall, saying, "Don't worry, I have my own considerations."

Putting aside the deduction of the new method for now, Bai Shuyue, relying on the power of Wuji, disregarded the paradoxical rules of the Heaven-Bestowing Celestial Venerables within the Xuanhuang Realm and focused on cultivation.

As for resources, with the Boundless Wall's sponsorship, there was no need to worry.

The terrifying spirituality accumulated over thirty reincarnations was now revealed.

After more than ten days of seclusion, Bai Shuyue felt an invisible barrier blocking her path.

Ahead...

There was no longer a way forward.

"According to ancient immortal cultivation texts, I have now reached the end of the mortal realm, the pinnacle of Dao integration."

"If seeking immortality is hopeless, one can only seek the method of eternal life. Either by borrowing the power of the rules of heaven and earth, or by relying on treasures that extend life."

"A realm that countless cultivators throughout history have dreamed of, I have reached in ten days."

"Cultivation... is unexpectedly easy."

Bai Shuyue looked up at the sky and let out a long sigh.

Honestly, even she herself had not expected her cultivation speed to be so terrifying.

So much so that after emerging from seclusion, she was momentarily unable to react.

Let alone the Boundless Wall.

The Boundless Wall, which had been constantly urging her to cultivate quickly, fell into an unusual silence after witnessing Bai Shuyue's cultivation speed without any restrictions.

It even seemed somewhat frightened, quietly distancing itself, no longer as close as before. Bai Shuyue found it amusing: "You went to great lengths to make me cultivate, and now that I've truly worked hard, you're acting like this."

"What is the reason?"

The Boundless Wall mumbled, "I just suddenly thought of something frightening."

"Frightened?"

"Perhaps, in other worlds, you are an incredibly important figure, but in this world, you are suppressed by the special environment of the Xuanhuang Realm, and thus have always remained a mortal. If you meet your other selves from other worlds after transcending..."

"Wouldn't that be like a lamb falling into a tiger's den?"

The more the Boundless Wall thought about it, the more it felt it made sense, its expression becoming increasingly gloomy, like an eggplant left out in the frost, sighing.

"How could it be like a lamb falling into a tiger's den?" Bai Shuyue suddenly became curious.

At this moment, she did not yet understand the truth of the Mountains and Seas, the parallel existence of countless possibilities, let alone the cultivation method of transcended beings unifying themselves.

The Boundless Wall briefly explained, and then Bai Shuyue's expression became solemn.

"In that case, even after transcending, it might not necessarily be safer? Instead, it might become more dangerous due to exposure?"

"Under normal circumstances, those who reach the realm of transcendence have no need to worry about these things. After all, even if there are infinite possibilities in the Mountains and Seas, it is extremely rare for multiple selves to achieve transcendence. But with your monstrous talent, it's hard to say." The Boundless Wall sighed.

Having high cultivation talent was indeed a good thing, but if it was too high, it was not necessarily a good thing.

This was something the Boundless Wall had not considered before.

However, as someone who knew herself, Bai Shuyue was well aware of the source of her heaven-defying talent.

"Other selves in other worlds, if they don't have the ability to reincarnate continuously, would certainly not be like me." Bai Shuyue understood in her heart and felt slightly relieved.

Even though she knew the truth, she could not explain it to the Boundless Wall and simply maintained an awkward silence.

In the midst of the silence, Bai Shuyue couldn't help but let her thoughts wander again, becoming curious about the truth behind her continuous reincarnation.

"If I keep reincarnating, what about the other me in other worlds?"

"What kind of power is controlling all of this?"

When she was still a mortal, Bai Shuyue did not feel much fear towards her ability to reincarnate continuously, considering it merely a lucky gift.

But now, as she was just one step away from becoming an immortal, she saw everything more clearly because of it.

This was a divine power that even True Immortals could not reach or imagine!

The more she thought about it, the more she felt her own insignificance and powerlessness before it.

The Boundless Wall and Bai Shuyue both fell into a long silence, but their fears were not the same.

After a long while, it was finally the Boundless Wall that broke the silence.

"Damn it, I still believe my intuition. This should not be a misfortune, but a great blessing!" he gritted his teeth and said.

Bai Shuyue snapped back to reality, shaking her head with a sneer, "Let's take it one step at a time. I was originally just a mere mortal, and now I have lived so many more lifetimes. Even if I die abruptly, it will have been worth it. Why think about so many things for no reason?"

After this interlude, the two officially discussed the method of ascending to immortality.

"Immortals, unlike mortals, possess the power of [Mountains and Seas]."

"The mountains above impart the high status of immortals; the infinite sea provides endless sources..."

Like any other being hearing the truth of Mountains and Seas for the first time, Bai Shuyue's mind was deeply shaken. The power of immortals far exceeded their imagination.

"No wonder the difference between immortals and mortals is like a chasm," Bai Shuyue marveled internally.

The Boundless Wall continued, "Your current realm is sufficient. If the Immortal Court were still in existence, and I could secretly obtain an Imperial Decree from the Immortal Emperor, I could make you an immortal on the spot. However..."

"The Immortal Realm is destroyed, and even with the supplies in my belly, they are probably useless now."

Bai Shuyue was somewhat puzzled, "How much have you swallowed into your belly?"

The Boundless Wall rolled its large eye, "What do you know? Having provisions in my belly gives me peace of mind. If it weren't for my foresight in hoarding so many supplies in the past, how could I have survived the great tribulation of the Immortal Realm's destruction?"

"Perhaps it's precisely because I've eaten too much that my current strength is not as good as before." Bai Shuyue scoffed.

"There is some truth to that. However..." The Boundless Wall's large eye rolled, speaking with some self-satisfaction, "The strength I pursue is actually quite different from you cultivators."

Having been together for so many lifetimes, this was the first time Bai Shuyue had heard the Boundless Wall say such a thing. After thinking about it, her eyes revealed disbelief, "Could it be that your ultimate pursuit is still..."

"That's right! My wish is to become the foremost pillar of talent in the Mountains and Seas!" the Boundless Wall declared solemnly.

To perform the act of slaying immortals with the body of a mortal should have been a fantastical tale.

Chapter 1638: The Empty and Profound Is But a Plaything

"Possessing a sharp weapon, the urge to kill arises."

Feeling the aura of Wuji surrounding her, Bai Shuyue's heart suddenly surged with heroic spirit.

True Immortals, Nameless True Immortals, so what?

With the body of a mortal, it is not impossible to slay them!

Yet, this Yin and Yang power is innate, nurtured from birth, and extremely difficult to resolve with later means. She must have a male companion who holds the other half of the Taiyang璞玉 to help her resolve it. Even if the other party is a mortal with no strength to even tie a chicken, she can only accept whatever is offered, or even actively seek intimacy."

The Boundless Wall examined Kong You, seeing through her secrets completely, "And, in this process, she cannot enjoy any of the benefits brought by the harmony of Yin and Yang. All of it goes to the other half. Tsk tsk tsk, the people of the Xuanhuang Realm play so well..."

"Enough!" The Boundless Wall was interrupted by Bai Shuyue's cold voice when it was halfway through speaking.

Although Bai Shuyue had lived alone for more than ten years and had long forgotten about romantic love, she was, after all, a woman and understood the torment such methods would bring to a woman.

"Kong You has reached the Dao Integration realm, yet she is still treated as a plaything. I imagine the person behind this must be one of those Law-Imparting Elders from the Myriad Immortal Alliance. Hmph!" Having worked in the Myriad Immortal Alliance for several lifetimes, Bai Shuyue immediately understood the whole story.

While the two were communicating telepathically, Kong You, who had been inexplicably blocked, had already made her move.

Renowned among the cultivators of the Myriad Immortal Alliance, Kong You did not rely solely on her beauty. Her own strength was also not to be underestimated.

With the accompanying Taiyin power, combined with the Dao Integration soul, [Youse], she wove thousands of obscure threads, harming and trapping enemies invisibly.

Taiyin is endless and inexhaustible. Even in a frontal attrition, few in the same realm could match her.

But...

She could not withstand a single strike from Bai Shuyue.

This was the first time Bai Shuyue had actively attempted to manipulate the power of the Great Dao of Wuji to fight an enemy.

As if her abilities were suddenly stripped away, the power of Taiyin suddenly abandoned her, and the thousands of threads woven by Kong You slowly dispersed in the air.

Her hidden figure was also stunned and exposed.

Bai Shuyue struck her directly on the forehead.

Dizzy and disoriented, she fell to the ground in a sorry state.

Bai Shuyue looked at Kong You, put away the jade staff, but her expression slowly softened. She began to inquire about the identity of the person tormenting her behind the scenes.

However, Kong You's words made Bai Shuyue's killing intent surge several times over.

"I do not know his specific identity either. Because, each time the half of the Yang Jade comes, the identity is different."

"Sometimes it is a noble son of a prestigious family, sometimes an unknown minor cultivator, sometimes a beggar. Or, possibly like me, a woman..."

"And no matter what their cultivation level is, I am powerless to resist."

"Who would have known that the famous Fairy Kong You of the Myriad Immortal Alliance was actually like this? Perhaps, this is what he truly desired." Kong You did not cry, as if she had long accepted her fate, quietly narrating.

After hearing the full account, not only was Bai Shuyue unable to accept it, but even the Boundless Wall was dumbfounded, "My heavens, the mortals of the Xuanhuang Realm truly have wonderful methods!"

A moment later, feeling Bai Shuyue's cold gaze, the Boundless Wall quickly changed its tune, "To toy with people's hearts like this is truly deserving of death!"

Despite being indignant, Bai Shuyue did not lose her composure. After having the Boundless Wall investigate again and confirm Kong You's words were true, Bai Shuyue finally made up her mind.

"The next person to test our skills will be the one behind this."

"Can you help me find out who it is?"

Sensing Bai Shuyue's unprecedented rage, the Boundless Wall dared not refuse in the slightest.

After helping Kong You retrieve the half piece of Yang Jade from her body, it swallowed it whole.

After a while, it spat out a complete piece and handed it to Kong You.

"Little lady, reabsorb this Jade. With your cultivation level, you will achieve a perfect balance of Yin and Yang within a few years. From then on, you will no longer be controlled by others!" The Boundless Wall coughed lightly and said in a slow voice.

Kong You seemed to not dare to believe it. Tremblingly, she took the Yang Jade, feeling the familiar yet strange complete aura. Even after enduring countless hardships, she had never shed a tear, but now, she cried.

Solemnly putting away the Yang Jade, she kowtowed to thank the two for their life-saving grace.

"Hmm, it feels good to be a good person occasionally. Although I lost some of the supplies in my belly." The Boundless Wall shook its head and beard.

"No longer being controlled by others..." Bai Shuyue mused internally about the sentence the Boundless Wall had inadvertently spoken.

As if it had touched some spiritual intuition, she vaguely felt as if she were about to touch upon a truth.

Unfortunately, the feeling flashed by and disappeared before she could grasp it.

The Boundless Wall's next words pulled her back to reality, "With the method of Yin-Yang Jade restraint broken, and the carefully crafted toy damaged, the person behind it will surely be furious and come looking for us. We just need to wait patiently here."

"What if he doesn't come?" Bai Shuyue's brows furrowed.

"Uh..." The Boundless Wall glanced at Bai Shuyue and immediately produced a backup plan.

"While refining the Yang Jade, I captured a trace of the original owner's aura. Although it's only a trace, we can follow the vine to find the person behind it. It will just be a bit troublesome!"

"Even if we have to search the entire Xuanhuang Realm, we must find this person. Otherwise, my resolve will be unsettled!" Bai Shuyue clenched the jade staff in her hand and issued a strict order.

The Boundless Wall sighed secretly and could only agree.

As Bai Shuyue had predicted, the hidden figure behind this unexpectedly did not come looking for Kong You for a long time, unlike someone whose beloved toy had been broken and who would be furious.

"Search!"

Bai Shuyue gave the order, and the Boundless Wall had to work hard.

Fortunately, the Xuanhuang Realm was not that large. After some effort, the Boundless Wall quickly locked onto the target.

"It points to this spatial void, it seems to be the headquarters of the Myriad Immortal Alliance."

"Can we break in?" Bai Shuyue asked with a frown.

She knew that the headquarters of the Myriad Immortal Alliance was actually within the Tianxuan Mirror, and it was protected by the Heavenly Venerable restrictions, making it extremely difficult to invade.

"That's no problem for me! Although this Xuanhuang Treasure Mirror is considered a top-grade immortal artifact, how can it compare to the gate of our Saint Monarch?" the Boundless Wall said proudly.

However, it soon sighed quietly, "But, I will have to use up some of my supplies again."

"Don't worry, I'll find some treasures later to feed you." Bai Shuyue shot the Boundless Wall a glance.

With Bai Shuyue's guarantee, the Boundless Wall felt relieved.

A streak of green light enveloped it, like lightning, tearing through space and instantly drilling into the crack.

"Oh, right, you just said it was the Xuanhuang Treasure Mirror? I remember the ultimate treasure of the Myriad Immortal Alliance should be called the Tianxuan Mirror." As they successfully infiltrated the Myriad Immortal Alliance headquarters and followed the faint connection of the Yin-Yang Jade to search for the person behind the scenes, Bai Shuyue suddenly realized something and asked.

"It is definitely the Xuanhuang Treasure Mirror. Although it has shed its immortality and become a mortal artifact, it cannot escape my clairvoyance. I just don't know why its name was changed. Logically speaking, the name of an immortal artifact and its spirit are intrinsically linked. They wouldn't change their name arbitrarily." The Boundless Wall was also somewhat puzzled and observed carefully within the internal space of the Tianxuan Mirror.

"Hmm?" After observing for a long time, it actually found some clues.

"This is, the technique of paper folding? A replica?" the Boundless Wall exclaimed.

"Paper folding?" Bai Shuyue looked at the Boundless Wall with some confusion.

"My Saint Monarch's grace. I didn't expect this person to also be in the Xuanhuang Realm." The Boundless Wall shivered and instinctively looked around, immediately falling silent.

Even though Bai Shuyue was extremely puzzled and repeatedly questioned, it did not say another word.

"It's not that I'm treating you as an outsider, but some matters are best left unsaid. Speaking too much leads to mistakes, and saying nothing leads to no mistakes. Knowing her will not do you any good. On the contrary, if you keep mentioning her, you might invite inexplicable disasters."

"Just like I don't mention the other one besides the three Saints of the Immortal Realm."

"Sometimes, it is precisely these powerful beings that are the most difficult to offend."

"After all, they are not true Saints like the Saint Monarch." the Boundless Wall explained intermittently.

Knowing that the Boundless Wall must have its difficulties, Bai Shuyue did not press further.

She could only suppress the anger in her heart towards the riddle-teller and continue to move forward.

In the Myriad Immortal Alliance headquarters, layers upon layers, countless spaces were engraved.

If fully unfolded, it would be hundreds of times vaster than the Xuanhuang Realm.

The place where the Law-Imparters resided was in the deepest part.

To avoid unnecessary trouble if discovered, the Boundless Wall moved cautiously all the way.

Finally, after traversing into a small, artificially created world, it finally found the person behind this.

"It looks like an ordinary world. The master here doesn't seem like someone who plays such elaborate games..." the Boundless Wall mumbled.

"But I'm definitely not mistaken!" Sensing Bai Shuyue's displeased gaze, the Boundless Wall immediately clarified.

"It's right there!"

A streak of blue light descended, enveloping a young cultivator below.

Being attacked by an outsider in his own world.

This Law-Imparter was instantly shocked and enraged.

But upon seeing Kong You among the three attackers, he suddenly calmed down.

"Tsk tsk tsk, I never expected you, my toy, to actually have some capabilities. Not only did you escape my control, but you even came to my doorstep?"

"What? Are you here to seek revenge?" the young Law-Imparter said somewhat flippantly, his tone laced with mockery.

Finally seeing the person who controlled her, Kong You had countless words she wanted to say. But after hearing the young Law-Imparter's words, the only thing echoing in her mind was the word "toy."

She opened her mouth, but nothing came out.

"You still don't know your doom is upon you!" only Bai Shuyue said coldly.

"Death?" As if hearing something extremely amusing, the young Law-Imparter laughed wildly up to the sky.

"Ignorant juniors, do you know that we Law-Imparters have made great contributions in establishing the Immortal Alliance? We have received the reward of the Heavenly Venerables and obtained the body of immortality and indestructibility! How can you, with your mere abilities, expect to kill me? If we Law-Imparters could be so easily killed, how could the Myriad Immortal Alliance maintain its foundation for thousands of years..."

"He's stalling for time," the Boundless Wall said calmly, like watching a clown, seeing through the trick. "Perhaps he's waiting for some helper. In reality, his heart is far from as shocked as he appears."

"You..." the young Law-Imparter's expression changed drastically.

Bai Shuyue also snorted coldly, "I naturally know. The Law-Imparters, although decadent and gradually losing their humanity, are not fools. We have already reached this point, how could he underestimate us? Reinforcements..."

"I'm afraid there's no time!"

Before her words even fell, Bai Shuyue had already unleashed the Heaven-Overturning Staff and swung it towards the young Law-Imparter.

"Immortality and indestructibility... is merely being a parasite!"

The power of the Great Dao of Wuji enveloped the opponent.

All external rule connections were severed at this moment.

As if sensing something, the young Law-Imparter's face instantly showed terror.

The next moment...

Flesh and blood blurred, brain matter splattered.

"Disgusting!"

Bai Shuyue withdrew her hand, looking at the thoroughly dead young Law-Imparter, and finally let out a breath of relief.

The Boundless Wall, with its single large eye, scanned the bloody remains.

"I know why this Law-Imparter Feng didn't come earlier."

"He has too many toys like this. If one or two are broken, it doesn't matter. It's not worth him risking coming here personally."

Chapter 1639: The Mountains and Seas Do Not Spare Immortals

Hearing that there were many other toys like herself, the Law-Imparter Feng, had died, Kong You's face showed an extremely complex expression.

This was just for the Law-Imparter Feng alone. What about the other Law-Imparters of the Myriad Immortal Alliance?

In this Xuanhuang Realm, how many people, throughout their entire lives, were unable to escape the fate of being played with?

"It might be better if they were destroyed," Bai Shuyue suddenly said for no reason.

Under the power of the Great Dao of Wuji, the Law-Imparter Feng died without a sound. The blood splattered everywhere had already cooled. The other Law-Imparters and the Heavenly Venerables of Law-Imparting had no awareness of it.

But when Bai Shuyue withdrew the power of Wuji, the Myriad Immortal Alliance headquarters immediately fell into great turmoil.

"It's time to go," Bai Shuyue glanced at the Boundless Wall.

A green light enveloped the three of them, and they quietly fled as they had come.

There's no need to dwell on the events within the Myriad Immortal Alliance.

After placing Kong You in a safe location and continuing to search for opponents for trial, Bai Shuyue seemed somewhat dejected since their separation.

The Boundless Wall seemed puzzled: "Didn't we just defeat and kill that Law-Imparter Feng who was behind everything? Why are you still so unhappy?"

"And speaking of which, I really didn't expect you to stand up for that little female cultivator."

"In the world of cultivation, the weak are prey to the strong. There's nothing to be said about it. Even I have taken the lives of many people before. But..." Bai Shuyue frowned tightly.

"Killing instantly is different from tormenting and playing with someone like this."

"I don't know why, but after seeing Kong You's experience, I felt a nameless anger in my heart. It was as if..." Bai Shuyue carefully savored the emotions in her heart at that time.

Although the emotional turmoil had subsided now, the unprovoked impulse at that moment was too strong, and Bai Shuyue still seemed immersed in it.

As if about to grasp something crucial, at this moment, the Boundless Wall suddenly shouted, interrupting her thoughts.

"Not good, someone is chasing us!"

Before the words were even finished, a figure had already descended.

A frail-looking young man, with a hint of lingering prettiness on his face. He stared at Bai Shuyue and the Boundless Wall, his expression calm yet tinged with killing intent.

The moment Bai Shuyue saw the opponent, she couldn't help but think of her nephew. The Boundless Wall, on the other hand, seemed to have encountered other pillars of Taiyi Palace from the past.

The vigilance in their hearts dissipated.

But soon, strands of the Great Dao of Wuji surged, dispelling the strange power that enveloped them.

"Hiss, I almost fell for the deception by the opponent. This strange characteristic..." The Boundless Wall protected Bai Shuyue, creating distance between themselves and the suddenly appearing young man, carefully identifying him.

He vaguely felt that he had seen a similar characteristic in some immortal artifact in the Immortal Realm. However, as he resided in Taiyi Palace and had seen countless beings in the Immortal Realm, this immortal artifact was not particularly outstanding, so he could not recall it immediately.

"As expected, eating too much has damaged my brain!"

Compared to the Boundless Wall's rapid thoughts, Bai Shuyue was much calmer.

She once again summoned the jade staff in her hand, pointing it at the young man ahead, "Are you here to avenge the Law-Imparter Feng?"

"I was, but now I'm not." The youth's answer was beyond Bai Shuyue's expectations.

He smiled and cupped his hands, "With the cultivation of you two seniors, you must not have acted without reason. Feng must have done something that deserved death, so he died. He deserved it, why should I avenge him?"

Bai Shuyue snorted coldly, her expression softening slightly.

"However, I am under the command of the Heavenly Venerable and manage all affairs of the Myriad Immortal Alliance. Feng was one of the Law-Imparters, and his sudden death like this, although it has no substantial impact on the stability of the Myriad Immortal Alliance, I must provide an explanation if the Heavenly Venerable asks..." The young man bowed respectfully to Bai Shuyue and the Boundless Wall.

"I hope the two seniors can tell me the cause and effect."

Bai Shuyue hesitated for a moment, feeling that what the other party said made some sense, so she explained the incident and questioned him, "Is this how you manage the affairs of the Myriad Immortal Alliance? Speaking of which, with your status, who are you..."

"Law-Imparter Jiang?"

Bai Shuyue narrowed her eyes slightly, recalling this Law-Imparter whose name she had heard but never seen.

"Oh, I remember him, it's him!"

"No wonder, even with the protection of the Great Dao of Wuji, we were still slightly affected by him." The Boundless Wall exclaimed again.

Hearing this, Bai Shuyue finally jolted awake.

In front of this stranger, she had been too talkative.

Just moments ago, they were adversaries in a life-and-death struggle, but after a few words from the other party, her inner vigilance had dissipated. And without much deliberation, she had agreed to his request...

The Boundless Wall quickly explained the origin of Law-Imparter Jiang's "Everything is like me" characteristic.

"A long time ago, there was a 'Hundred Knowledge Immortal' in the Immortal Realm, who claimed to know all the secrets of the Immortal Realm from ancient times to the present. Even the secrets of Immortal Emperors and Saint Monarchs were not beyond his knowledge. At first, the Immortals thought his words were exaggerated, but after the Hundred Knowledge Immortal wrote a secret record of the Immortal Emperors, the entire Immortal Realm believed him!"

"Immortal Emperor's Secret Record? What is that?"

"If I knew, I'm afraid I wouldn't be able to lie peacefully on the roof of Taiyi Palace! It probably contains forbidden content related to the private lives of successive Immortal Emperors. Once released, it caused the Immortal Emperors to fly into a rage and order the Hundred Knowledge Immortal to be captured."

"This action by the Hundred Knowledge Immortal even angered a hidden Emperor who had been in seclusion for many years. He personally took action and forcibly erased the Immortal Emperor's Secret Record, which had already spread among the Immortals, from everyone's memories..."

"What happened afterward?" Bai Shuyue asked.

"What else could happen? His extremely arrogant behavior even angered the Saint Monarch. Naturally, he was captured and suppressed. After this, people finally understood the reason why the Hundred Knowledge Immortal was called [Hundred Knowledge]."

"It wasn't because of the Great Dao he cultivated, but because he forged an immortal artifact called [As If Intent]!"

"Living beings are cautious and guarded towards others. But towards themselves, they have no such defenses. It was precisely by using this principle that the Hundred Knowledge Immortal spent the first half of his life gathering countless treasures, finally forging such a heaven-defying immortal artifact, [As If Intent]!"

"We beings, traversing the Great Dao, are like shadows of the Great Dao. But As If Intent could do, [to make shadow imitate shadow]. To become the shadow of the Dao's shadow! Upon seeing it, one would instinctively feel a sense of closeness, even mistaking it for themselves... It was extremely bizarre, even Immortal Emperors couldn't escape it. The shadow of As If Intent covered almost the entire Immortal Realm, seeing through everyone's hearts. Such malicious behavior was universally condemned, resulting in no one willing to speak on his behalf after he was suppressed. After many years, his deeds have almost completely disappeared from people's minds."

"If I weren't so knowledgeable, I would have almost forgotten this past event."

The Boundless Wall slowly narrated a past event.

However, Bai Shuyue listened with a frown.

"Are you sure you haven't misremembered?"

"Misremember? How is that possible? Don't question my memory!" The Boundless Wall finally called itself "this great master" again, showing great indignation at Bai Shuyue's doubt.

"But I always feel that there are many inconsistencies in the story you told, and even some parts are contradictory..." Bai Shuyue murmured.

"Illusion, it's definitely your illusion!?" the Boundless Wall swore confidently.

Bai Shuyue, however, firmly believed in her judgment: "The shadow of the Dao's shadow, everything is like me. This ability is indeed unbelievable, but what does it have to do with the name [As If Intent]? You said you angered the Saint Monarch, and although I haven't seen the Saint Monarch's true face, I know he possesses supreme divine abilities and cultivation. Even the destruction of multiple possibilities might not be taken seriously by the Saint Monarch. Why would such a trivial matter as leaking secrets infuriate the Saint Monarch? And..."

"Be careful, be careful." After Bai Shuyue's reminder, the Boundless Wall also slowly realized the inaccuracies in its memory. But it quickly realized something and hurriedly stopped Bai Shuyue from continuing.

"I understand now, this Hundred Knowledge Immortal, is [Vast Seas Unforgiven]!"

"Vast Seas Unforgiven?"

"A person so wicked that even after the Dao Annihilation crisis ends and the Mountains and Seas are rebuilt, they will not be forgiven! It is said that they will be eternally suppressed in the depths of the Shore, never to be free for generations..." the Boundless Wall whispered.

"Good heavens, how did a shard of an immortal artifact made by such a villain fall into the Xuanhuang Realm? This is not good. Let me think, why did I escape to the Xuanhuang Realm back then? Was it truly a coincidence?" The Boundless Wall muttered to itself with some fear.

Bai Shuyue, however, was still contemplating, unable to connect [As If Intent] with the grave crime of Vast Seas Unforgiven.

Meanwhile, Law-Imparter Jiang, who had been observing the shifting expressions and constant glances cast upon him by the two, felt an inexplicable sense of unease.

He said sternly, "Seniors, please rest assured. I already know the details of this matter. Feng indeed deserved to die, and even failed in his duty as a Law-Imparter. The Myriad Immortal Alliance will not pursue this matter. Instead, we should thank the seniors for helping the Myriad Immortal Alliance eliminate a corrupt element!"

"Seniors, you were right earlier. I have indeed been too lenient with other Law-Imparters. Perhaps I should truly reorganize things..."

After saying this, Law-Imparter Jiang prepared to hastily take his leave.

"Wait!" the Boundless Wall called out to him.

"Let me take a good look first!"

Law-Imparter Jiang did not know what the Boundless Wall wanted to look at so intently.

But it was likely related to his innate special ability.

Although he instinctively wanted to refuse, a streak of green light enveloped him.

His heart startled, and he tried to resist with magic, but it was like sinking into mud; he could not exert any of his strength.

He could only let the other party gaze upon him.

Law-Imparter Jiang secretly lamented, but there was nothing he could do. He could only try his best to activate his "Everything is like me" characteristic to its extreme, in order to protect himself.

"What are you looking for?" Bai Shuyue asked the Boundless Wall, which was acting so mysteriously.

"I'm looking to see if I can find the memories of his arrival in the Xuanhuang Realm from this fellow." The Boundless Wall's green light continued to swirl.

Its tone had also become unprecedentedly serious.

"I always thought that the Young Master and I coming to the Xuanhuang Realm at the same time was a coincidence. But recently, while searching the vast Xuanhuang Realm with you, I have discovered traces of so many incredible powers..."

"And this is just the tip of the iceberg! It's truly unimaginable what else this small Xuanhuang Realm hides. That's why I was afraid and had to confirm it again."

The green light flickered, displaying the entire life of Law-Imparter Jiang.

From the thousands of years he presided over the Myriad Immortal Alliance, to when he was a mere mortal disciple of a Law-Imparter, and even to his ignorant childhood. Although not every detail was revealed, most of his past was exposed to Bai Shuyue and the Boundless Wall.

Although Law-Imparter Jiang could not move, his face turned extremely red with shame and indignation.

However, the instigator, the Boundless Wall, was completely uninterested in these facts.

The green light continued to shine, attempting to probe even further back into the past.

The time spent on this investigation was longer than all the previous ones combined.

The Boundless Wall seemed to become "thinner" due to the immense consumption.

Finally, a change occurred.

The tide of Dao Annihilation swallowed Mountains and Seas; the Immortal Realm shattered, immortals died like ants.

Terrible scenes unfolded, as if the Boundless Wall was transported back to the time of the great tribulation.

The Boundless Wall's body trembled uncontrollably with fear.

The Immortal Realm was shattered, but a fragment of light broke free from its prison.

Like a meteor, it streaked across the disintegrating Immortal Realm, doing its best to avoid the constantly approaching tide of Dao Annihilation.

After constant traversal, it finally escaped the Immortal Realm and arrived in the lower star seas.

However, as if sensing something or being attracted by something.

While fleeing through the lower star seas, it suddenly changed direction.

Towards the direction of Xuanhuang!

During this period, as if to evade pursuit, the flowing light itself underwent changes.

Until it finally merged into the tiny Xuanhuang Realm.

...

*Buzz!* With a humming sound, the green light exploded.

A nearly invisible crack appeared on the Boundless Wall, and the Boundless Wall emerged from the reconnaissance.

Law-Imparter Jiang, now free, fled in a panic.

Bai Shuyue was about to pursue him, but was stopped by the Boundless Wall's frantic voice.

"Don't go after him, run quickly!"

"Run? Run from what?" Bai Shuyue was slightly stunned.

"Disaster has struck. My probing of the past scenes of the Immortal Realm's destruction has attracted the attention of Dao Annihilation! The tide of world destruction is coming!"

As if confirming the Boundless Wall's words, at this moment, the Xuanhuang Realm, the Utmost Dark Star Sea, and even the entire Primordial Possibility,

All fell into a brief silence.

A moment later, like a vast tidal roar, a deafening roar faintly came from beyond the heavens.

As if dark clouds were pressing down, the sky turned eerily black.

And all living beings felt a sense of impending doom in their hearts.

"This feeling, this feeling..."

"Run quickly!" the Boundless Wall screamed.

But it was already too late.

For some unknown reason, the Dao Annihilation triggered by the Boundless Wall's probe into Law-Imparter Jiang's past memories was unprecedentedly powerful.

It even surpassed that caused by Li Fan.

Even the illusory projection left by Shou Qiu could not withstand it.

And it arrived so quickly.

The high walls shattered, the Primordial collapsed.

Including Bai Shuyue and the Boundless Wall, the living beings of the Primordial Possibility were instantly swallowed.

Only Li Fan's figure managed to escape in time.

Chapter 1640: Shan Hai Immortal Outer Heaven

"Vast Seas Unforgiven Immortal? What the heck is that..."

This Dao Annihilation came so abruptly that even Li Fan, who had been watching from the sidelines, barely had time to react and nearly perished in the Dao Annihilation.

However, having experienced great storms, Li Fan quickly calmed down after his narrow escape.

"I didn't expect the Law-Imparter Jiang's [Everything is like me] ability to have such an origin."

With Li Fan's experience, he could tell at a glance that the Hundred Knowledge Immortal and the immortal artifact [As If Intent] were definitely not as simple as the Boundless Wall initially described.

"Angering the Saint Monarch, Vast Seas Unforgiven. Could it be that this Hundred Knowledge Immortal is one of the two beings suppressed in the Eternal Heavenly Steps of the Shore?"

"And this strange reaction from the Dao Annihilation tide..."

In his past numerous close encounters with Dao Annihilation, Li Fan had already confirmed that Dao Annihilation possessed some kind of consciousness. Or rather, if Dao Annihilation were regarded as an army marching forth, then there was a supreme commander orchestrating the Dao Annihilation's attack.

The army advanced, infiltrating everywhere; encircling and slowly eroding; encountering fierce resistance, it would dispatch more troops to crush the enemy completely...

But this time, upon sensing something related to the Hundred Knowledge Immortal, Dao Annihilation's reaction was different from usual.

"It's as if a scout discovered the trace of the target enemy, and instantly summoned the main force, pointing straight towards it!"

Li Fan felt a sudden shock in his heart.

"The essence of Dao Annihilation is the merging of Mountains and Seas. It is the return of the power of [Stars], transcending the Mountains and Seas. Merely a fragment of an immortal artifact forged by it could cause such a commotion in Dao Annihilation."

"In a sense, could this Hundred Knowledge Immortal, or rather, [As If Intent], be worthy of the attention of [Stars]?"

Instinctively, Li Fan felt a sense of curiosity.

"Perhaps I can glean more from Law-Imparter Jiang's memories, but there is no longer an opportunity in this lifetime."

"Let's wait for the contest of virtue and reason."

Li Fan glanced towards the area beyond the Mountains and Seas.

Instead of seeking refuge in other possibilities, he simply unfurled a lone sail of Mountains and Seas and drifted on the tide of Dao Annihilation.

Feeling the surging undercurrents of Dao Annihilation, Li Fan had a realization: "Even though the Primordial was destroyed, they have not given up their search. This means that in the eyes of Dao Annihilation, the annihilation tide of this magnitude could not possibly kill the Hundred Knowledge Immortal. If he disappeared, it must be because he had once again escaped and hidden."

"To evoke such emotional turmoil among the Saints of the Shore, he is truly extraordinary!"

Li Fan recalled his encounters with the Saints in his simulations. They always appeared calm and confident. Even when observing Huizhen and his own infinite spirituality, it was the same.

It was hard to imagine what the Hundred Knowledge Immortal had done to warrant the Saints personally intervening to suppress him.

"The source of Law-Imparter Jiang's power, that fragment that escaped during the destruction of the Immortal Realm, is perhaps only one of the remaining traces of the Hundred Knowledge Immortal on the Shore."

"Even so, Dao Annihilation attaches such importance to it."

From all this, it could be inferred that the Hundred Knowledge Immortal, or [As If Intent], might play a crucial role in this great battle between Dao Annihilation and the Mountains and Seas.

Not to mention deciding the outcome, but at the very least, it could significantly alter the current stalemate.

"In that case, this Hundred Knowledge Immortal is also unique to the Primordial."

"What's going on? Is this possibility I'm in becoming too outrageous?"

"Not only Shou Qiu, a Saint, but also such a character?"

"Plus Jing Fen, Dao De, King Xuanyuan and his wife..."

"Not to mention me, and Huizhen."

"They said a single possibility has an upper limit on potential. So why..."

Li Fan frowned deeply.

"Perhaps, I picked the right name for the Primordial back then by accident?"

But observing the facts, it was contrary to this.

The Primordial Immortal Realm had long been destroyed. The remaining parts had been destroyed countless times.

There was nothing special about it.

"Where exactly did things go wrong?" Li Fan keenly sensed the deep contradiction.

Everything seemed so strange and disharmonious.

"Theoretically, in times of prosperity, talent emerges. If the foundation of a single possibility is sufficient to produce so many powerhouses of the Mountains and Seas, then that possibility cannot be too weak. But now..."

Li Fan drifted with the current, floating on the Dao Annihilation, hidden within the Mountains and Seas, contemplating this issue.

Within the Mountains and Seas, time does not pass.

After an unknown period, after witnessing the scenes of other nearby possibilities where the Immortal Realm was still prosperous, Li Fan was enlightened. A thought flashed through his mind.

"I always thought the Primordial Immortal Realm had been destroyed long ago. But..."

"What if, I say, what if Emperor Tian Luo's plan had succeeded?"

Emperor Tian Luo's method of saving the world was to promote the Immortal Realm to a higher level. Transcending the Mountains and Seas would naturally allow them to escape Dao Annihilation. Although it sounded like fantasy, the original plan was unknown.

The fact that he had many supporters at the time suggested that the plan had some feasibility.

Even if the probability was extremely small, it was not entirely impossible for it to succeed.

If Emperor Tian Luo had truly achieved transcendence with the Primordial Immortal Realm at that time...

Then the anomalies of the Primordial would seem reasonable.

After transcending, the foundation of the entire possibility would undergo a qualitative change, benefiting countless beings in the Primordial. Even the "residue" shed during the transcendence process would have an impact. Mediocre talents would become geniuses, and geniuses would become peerless geniuses.

Surpassing the ordinary, standing out from the crowd. Those who originally had an upper limit of only Nameless True Immortal could now aspire to the realm of transcendence.

"Perhaps, this is the only way to explain why the small Primordial produced so many transcendent beings."

"And..."

"I should have realized long ago that the Mountains and Seas are boundless, with infinite possibilities, and that [Huizhen]'s choice of [Primordial] might not have been a coincidence."

"What is special is not only the Xuanhuang Realm, but also the [Primordial]."

"It is precisely by inheriting the resources of the Primordial that the small Xuanhuang Realm appears to be so full of hidden dragons and crouching tigers."

Thinking of this, as if clearing away the fog, Li Fan felt that everything suddenly became smooth.

Although all of this was his "guess" so far.

But Li Fan's intuition, like a guiding light, told him at the moment he "guessed" it, that this might be the truth.

"It's truly incredible."

Faced with this outcome, Li Fan himself fell silent for a long time.

Precisely because it was too bizarre, even though there were already various signs before, Li Fan had not thought in this direction.

Nor did he dare to think so.

However, by chance in this lifetime, upon learning of the existence of the [Hundred Knowledge Immortal], Li Fan suddenly broke through his previous limitations.

But greater questions followed.

"Who exactly is Emperor Tian Luo?"

"Where did the Primordial Immortal Realm go after successfully transcending?"

Chapter 1641: The End of the World Saint

Perhaps only a Saint Sovereign could know the answer to this question.

With Li Fan's current strength and realm, overthinking was futile.

Concealing himself within the Dao Annihilation, observing everything around him, Li Fan let out a long sigh until this disturbance completely subsided: "The Mountains and Seas are boundless. Beyond the Mountains and Seas, are there still Mountains and Seas?"

"What indeed are Stars and Creator Gods?"

At this moment, Li Fan stood outside the ruins of the Primordial, as if he had returned to the wreckage of Xuanjing City, gazing upon the grand scenery of the cultivation world for the first time with mortal eyes.

"That moment, just like this moment."

"Only now, I am no longer the simple Grand Tutor Li of the past!"

"Even if my body is like an ant, I possess the power to overturn the heavens and earth."

This was the absolute confidence Li Fan had gained through countless cycles of reincarnation, after understanding True Reversion, understanding his own infinite spirituality, and understanding intuition.

"The path ahead is distant and vague, but I will surely walk it to its end."

"Neither Dao nor Saint Venerable can stop me in the slightest."

"Let's give it a try!"

There was no grand declaration, nor any fiery passion.

Li Fan simply uttered these words faintly in his heart.

Only the remaining Mountains and Seas served as witness.

...

In order to probe the secret of achieving Sainthood through Dao and Virtue, Li Fan spent the next period selecting a remote region of possibility clusters, far from the Shore and outside the influence of the Taiwei Sacred Dynasty.

He then scattered his True Spirit Seeds everywhere.

"Such a vast expanse of Mountains and Seas, burning up in an instant. If one were trapped within without knowledge, it would be truly difficult to distinguish truth from falsehood, and one would only feel that the end of the Mountains and Seas had truly arrived." Li Fan narrowed his eyes, mentally simulating the scenario and nodding with satisfaction.

"I just don't know how Ho Gan Immortal is preparing."

Although they did not have a pre-arranged method of contact between them, Li Fan had deliberately left a trace of his aura before departing.

If Ho Gan Immortal had made up his mind, he would surely contact him following that trace.

"Let's wait a bit longer."

"If he hesitates for too long..."

"Perhaps I can find the Faceless Immortal again."

In Li Fan's hand, the stone statue of the Faceless Immortal from the Xuanhuang Realm was enveloped by flowing silver light.

Although the Dao Annihilation tide of the Primordial had arrived suddenly, the Immortal of Dao and Virtue would not perish so easily.

The lingering traces of immortal power within the Faceless Immortal statue were proof of this.

"When the Primordial collapsed, and I escaped, I seemed to see two or three other figures. However, amidst the Dao Annihilation tide, escaping was paramount, and I did not see them clearly."

"And there probably wasn't the Faceless Immortal among them. This brat..."

"Truly cautious!"

Ho Gan Immortal did not make Li Fan wait too long.

One day, while Li Fan was still drifting within the Mountains and Seas, his heart suddenly stirred.

He then transmitted his current location.

After waiting for a moment on the spot, a wisp of black and white energy traversed the Dao Annihilation and descended before him.

Compared to the last time he saw him, Ho Gan Immortal's own flow of Virtue had progressed further.

He was not far from Li Fan's previous state as a Saint of Virtue!

"It seems you have made up your mind and resolved the remaining conscience within your heart beforehand. Decisive!" Li Fan observed and praised slowly.

"It's either success or death." Ho Gan Immortal cupped his hands, his expressionless.

"Before the grand plan begins, I still have a question in my heart. Can Fellow Daoist enlighten me?"

"Speak freely." Li Fan smiled.

"Fellow Daoist, are you a Saint?" Ho Gan Immortal stared intently at Li Fan, his gaze burning.

It was as if he wanted to engrave Li Fan's every subtle expression into his memory.

"Saint?" Li Fan chuckled disdainfully and spoke frankly: "Fellow Daoist, you are thinking too much."

"If I were a Saint, I'm afraid that the [Virtue] of the Mountains and Seas would not be left for the two of you to attain!"

Li Fan's words did not seem like a lie. Upon receiving the answer, Ho Gan Immortal let out a long sigh.

But then, his brows furrowed, and a greater doubt surged in his heart.

"I know your doubts. Why do I understand you two so well? And why do I possess this divine ability to burn the Mountains and Seas?"

"I cannot reveal the specific details. However..."

"There are mountains beyond mountains, and seas beyond seas. Whatever happens in the world is not surprising," Li Fan said calmly.

These words had a greater impact on Ho Gan Immortal's heart than the revelation that Li Fan might be a Saint of the Mountains and Seas.

"Mountains beyond mountains? What does that mean?"

"Could it be that this person comes from another Mountain and Sea?"

His heart trembling violently, Ho Gan Immortal's mind flashed with the ancient legend in an instant.

The reason why Saints were willing to go one after another, traversing the Eternal Void, to the previous segment of Mountains and Seas, and jointly undertake the mission of saving the Mountains and Seas with countless cultivators from different time periods.

It was originally because someone had come from the end of the Mountains and Seas, defying the current and crossing over.

It ignited hope.

If not for this initial spark, the beings of the Mountains and Seas might still be fighting amongst themselves, without a unified goal.

"Perhaps this person comes from a different time of the Mountains and Seas?"

"Having crossed the Eternal Void several times, this could explain why his True Spirit is almost inexhaustible."

"Moreover, meeting for the first time, he directly claims to help me achieve Sainthood..."

In Ho Gan Immortal's mind, countless thoughts flashed by.

All the previous doubts seemed to have a perfect explanation.

"Although only the Saint realm allows one to traverse the Eternal Void. But as he said, the Mountains and Seas are boundless. No matter what happens, it is not surprising. Perhaps he was severely injured during the crossing and fell from his realm."

"Or perhaps, what he said was that he is not a Saint from our spacetime Mountains and Seas."

"Regardless, the most crucial thing is..."

"Since this person is not a Saint, then I don't need to worry so much!"

His gaze finally became resolute, and Ho Gan Immortal cupped his hands again: "I have learned."

"Fellow Daoist, when will your plan be executed?"

"I am ready to confront the other party head-on." His tone was calm and fearless.

"So be it!"

"There's no time like the present!"

Li Fan said, tossing the stone statue of the Faceless Immortal from the Xuanhuang Realm over.

"With this object, can you track his whereabouts?"

A flicker of surprise flashed in Ho Gan Immortal's eyes: "Given his cautious nature, he actually left such a flaw."

The black and white energies of Dao and Virtue enveloped the stone statue of the Faceless Immortal.

Slowly, a face grew on the originally ethereal face of the statue.

It resembled Ho Gan Immortal somewhat, but was not entirely the same.

Instead, it was more similar to the Saint Sovereign of Virtue that Li Fan had seen in his previous life.

"He is me, and I am him. Although Dao and Virtue have split..."

"But with this medium, it is not difficult to connect with him."

"Hmph, refusing to speak? In that case..."

The black and white energies grew more concentrated.

Weaving into a mist.

Under the dense fog, the stone statue seemed to come alive.

It actually slowly turned its head and looked towards a certain direction in the Mountains and Seas.

Suddenly...

"How is this possible? He's already dead?"

Accompanying Ho Gan Immortal's incredulous cry, as if seeing something forbidden, the stone statue's head was also broken off simultaneously.

It fell to the ground!

Chapter 1642: The Instigator of the Same Origin

One stone stirred a thousand ripples!

The Annihilation of the Dao seemed to sense something, and the tide gradually rose.

Li Fan felt something was amiss, his spirit instantly tensed, fully prepared to enact his True Reversion.

Fortunately, this unknown, bizarre power only severed the stone statue's head. It did not take any further action.

Ho Gan Immortal, while startled, touched his own neck.

At the moment the stone statue's head fell, he felt as if he too had suffered an attack.

Ho Gan Immortal's flow of Virtue was in near perfection. He considered himself only one step away from the realm of a Saint.

Yet, when facing that strange power just now, he felt as if he had no power to resist!

How was this possible?

Could it truly be the work of a Saint?

"Can you grasp its origin?" Li Fan asked with a solemn expression.

Ho Gan Immortal gazed into the mountains and seas for a long time before shaking his head and replying, "It came erratically and mysteriously... difficult."

"Besides, even if I could sense it, I wouldn't dare to delve deeper."

"My other self, though weaker in strength, still possesses the true intent of Virtue. His power transcends, it would be extremely difficult to kill him."

Ho Gan Immortal's words were clearly implied.

However, Li Fan held a different opinion.

Even a being like the Hundred Knowledge Immortal, who allegedly threatened the safety of the entire segment of mountains and seas, was not killed by the various Saints, but merely suppressed in the eternal heavenly steps.

The Faceless Immortal, though lacking virtue, had not committed any morally bankrupt acts to date.

The Saints had no reason to intervene.

"Then, besides them, who else in the mountains and seas possesses such strength?"

Both men fell into an eerie silence.

The sudden demise of the Faceless Immortal had completely shattered their plan.

Ho Gan Immortal's path to Sainthood was temporarily dashed, and Li Fan's intention to glimpse the true meaning of Virtue also fell through.

Faced with this unexpected turn of events, Li Fan, while inwardly shocked, also felt a surge of inexplicable anger.

"In my previous life, the Faceless Immortal lived quite well. This time, however, he died mysteriously. Is it related to the destruction of the Primordial or the Hundred Knowledge Immortal?"

"I can clearly still feel the remnants of his immortal power on the Faceless Immortal statue..."

Li Fan couldn't help but look at Ho Gan Immortal with suspicion.

As if sensing Li Fan's question, Ho Gan Immortal could only explain with helplessness: "The flow of Virtue is ceaseless; this is our divine ability to avoid calamity. Wherever we leave our traces, there will be residual traces of Virtue. It can be seen as a special clone we leave behind. If the main body unfortunately perishes, as long as there are still residual traces of Virtue in the mountains and seas, we can be reborn from the destruction."

"It should have been like this," Ho Gan Immortal said heavily, his expression extremely grim.

"Now, he is truly dead."

Ho Gan Immortal pointed to the beheaded stone statue: "Although there are still traces of Virtue flowing on it, it has lost its 'main backbone' that should have existed. Even if the Virtue can revive on its own in the future, he will not return."

"This is true death."

Li Fan vaguely heard a hint of shared sorrow and undeniable fear in Ho Gan Immortal's words.

After all, he and the Faceless Immortal were two sides of the same coin, separated from Virtue.

If that unknown, bizarre entity could kill the Faceless Immortal, it could also kill Ho Gan Immortal!

"Not only did it kill the main body, but it also erased it as an individual from the mountains and seas."

"All the preparations made in the past became irrelevant the moment he disappeared."

"This is a true method of annihilation!"

While the method was terrifying, what made Li Fan more uneasy was its sudden appearance without warning and its ethereal, untraceable nature.

Li Fan had traveled nearly a tenth of the mountains and seas. Coupled with the vast influence of the Saint Dynasty that spanned the mountains and seas, there were few unknown realms left for Li Fan.

He had thought so himself.

The sudden appearance of this bizarre force that killed the Faceless Immortal was a great shock.

It was like every time he thought he had fully explored the secrets of the Xuanhuang Realm, the Xuanhuang Realm would always give him another surprise.

More importantly...

This force seemed to be very close to him!

If the mountains and seas were seen as a gradually familiar pond, then now, a terrifying monster lurking in the depths of the water was ready to devour anyone.

How could this not make Li Fan feel shocked and angry?

The shock and anger were not due to the opponent's power, but the unknown.

The Saints of the Shore and Emperor Tai Wei were indeed powerful, but Li Fan could avoid them if he couldn't provoke them.

What he feared was this kind of entity, powerful enough to threaten Li Fan, yet always lurking in the shadows.

If he were unlucky, perhaps one day, while Li Fan was roaming the mountains and seas, he might stumble right into it.

Unable to suppress the unease in his heart, Li Fan made a decision: he would take advantage of the lingering clues from the Faceless Immortal's death to probe for more information about the perpetrator.

Not necessarily to fully ascertain the perpetrator's identity, but at the very least, to ascertain the general region they occupied in the mountains and seas. Before he had sufficient strength to avoid danger, he would try his best to steer clear of them.

With this thought, Li Fan looked at Ho Gan Immortal again and sneered, "You're scared?"

"The opportunity to become a Saint is right before your eyes, yet it was cut short by a single blow. As the saying goes, obstructing someone's path is like killing their parents."

"You two are one, two sides of Virtue. His death is like your own demise."

"The person who killed your entire family is right in front of you, and you choose to flee?"

"Do you not even have the courage to investigate?" Li Fan stared intently at Ho Gan Immortal.

Ho Gan Immortal fell into silence again, appearing quite hesitant.

"If that's the case, then forget I said anything. I didn't expect the legendary True Immortal of Virtue to be so timid," Li Fan tutted and shook his head.

"You don't need to provoke me. Rather, I'm somewhat curious why you are so concerned about this murderer?" Ho Gan Immortal countered.

"Firstly, it's because I'm upset about losing the opportunity to witness the birth of a Saint. Secondly..."

Li Fan's gaze was distant: "I suspect this murderer is someone I have been tracking."

This statement once again stirred the waves within Ho Gan Immortal's heart: "Are you so sure you can help me become a Saint?"

"If Virtue were complete, then it would be almost certain. But now... perhaps we have to wait for many years, for Virtue to flow and a new non-virtue to arise. I hope that by then, this segment of mountains and seas will still exist, and you will still be alive," Li Fan said with an enigmatic smile.

Having said that, his figure began to fade, about to depart.

"Wait!" At the last moment, Ho Gan Immortal suddenly called out.

"Let's try it for now. I hope that when the time is right again in the future, you can help me again!"

After all, the temptation of becoming a Saint was too great. Even if it was just a vague promise with no foundation, Ho Gan Immortal was unwilling to give it up just like that.

"Easily said! Rest assured, if there's an opportunity, I will definitely help you become a Saint!" Li Fan said proudly.

After reaching an agreement, Ho Gan Immortal looked at the severed head of the Faceless Immortal statue ahead and took a deep breath.

Then, he reached out and grabbed his own head, muttering in a low voice.

He actually tore his own head from his body.

"Please reside, precious one," Ho Gan Immortal's head, held in his own hands, glared with wide eyes, murmuring in a low voice.

Ho Gan Immortal's body, however, moved rather stiffly, step by step towards the Faceless Immortal statue.

Finally, holding his head with both hands with extreme solemnity, he placed it on the statue's glowing neck.

The stone body of the Faceless Immortal, Ho Gan Immortal's head.

The two, originally one, underwent extremely miraculous changes upon their combination.

It was as if the Faceless Immortal, who had been completely dead, was now resurrected!

As if experiencing everything the Faceless Immortal had gone through in his life, a trace of fear gradually appeared on Ho Gan Immortal's face.

His limbs flailed uncontrollably, as if instinctively trying to escape.

But whether in reality or in his memory, he could not escape.

Fear finally turned into despair.

"My life is forfeit!"

With a mournful cry, Ho Gan Immortal's head shot up into the sky, detaching from the statue.

The same scene replayed, but this time, a large amount of blood gushed out from the stone statue's body!

Only a moment later, the flying head regained clarity in its eyes.

While searching for his own body, it cried out: "Quick, run!"

Li Fan's reaction was even faster.

When he sensed the bizarre force striking again, severing Ho Gan Immortal's head, and even tending to spread to the surroundings. Li Fan had already ignited the True Spirit Fire seeds he had pre-buried in various possibilities.

Instantly, fierce flames raged, burning mountains and boiling seas!

Forming a terrifying wall of light and heat, it blocked the probing tentacles of power from the depths of the mountains and seas.

What was originally intended to trap the True Immortal of Virtue had now become a lifeline for saving them both.

And it was precisely because of this method that even though they were in extreme danger, Li Fan did not enact True Reversion.

The True Spirit Fire incinerated the possibilities, shattering them. With the loss of support, the tide of Dao Annihilation was immediately drawn in.

The surging water and fire swallowed the mountains and seas.

In an instant, it was like the end of the world!

At this moment, Ho Gan Immortal, who had found his separated body and reattached his head, regained his normal state and stared at the scene before him, stunned.

He never imagined that Li Fan would be so reckless!

Burning the vast mountains and seas as he pleased!

"What are you still愣住 for? The fire is raging, if you don't run now, when will you?"

Li Fan shouted lowly, pulling Ho Gan Immortal back to reality.

At that moment, without any hesitation, he instinctively followed Li Fan into the flames.

After all, the great fire was ignited with Li Fan's spiritual essence as fuel. With Li Fan leading the way, Ho Gan Immortal was not scorched by the True Spirit flames. He only managed to evade the successive waves of the Dao Annihilation tide.

Speeding out of the water and fire blockade, far from numerous possibilities, as the flames gradually receded, the sense of impending crisis in their hearts slowly dissipated. Only then did the two of them slow down.

Ho Gan Immortal looked back at the already burnt-out ruins of the mountains and seas, swallowed by the Dao Annihilation, still feeling the shock.

After a long while, he cupped his hands towards Li Fan and sighed with admiration: "If not for your decisive action, Fellow Daoist, igniting the mountains and seas, we truly would have been doomed this time."

Li Fan's expression turned solemn: "Do not slander me. When did I ever set fire to the mountains and seas in such a shocking and heinous manner? It was clearly the work of the villain who sought to kill us!"

Ho Gan Immortal was stunned by this remark.

Then he let out a wry laugh.

"Did you gain anything from suffering such a great tribulation?" Li Fan asked.

Ho Gan Immortal nodded, then shook his head.

"I used the method of unified Virtue to see a scene from my other self's life. But it was only for the last short period."

"And the assailant never truly revealed their form from beginning to end. Therefore..."

"Their true identity remains unknown."

"But I remembered the place of their demise and their aura. If we meet again, I will be alerted in advance."

This result was within Li Fan's expectations: "Can you point the direction?"

Ho Gan Immortal marked a location in the mountains and seas.

"It should be around here."

"Hmm?"

Li Fan stared at the fleeting phantom of mountains and seas before him and felt a sense of familiarity.

"This place is..."

Ho Gan Immortal also noticed Li Fan's strangeness and quickly asked, "Could it be that Fellow Daoist knows this place?"

Li Fan remained silent for a long time before slowly uttering a few words.

"Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation, Realm of Birth and Destruction!"

"Realm of Birth and Destruction?" Ho Gan Immortal repeated in a low voice. With his knowledge, he had never heard of such an existence within the mountains and seas.

He couldn't help but believe Li Fan's earlier statement that the murderer might be someone he had been tracking for a long time.

In fact, even after a hundred cycles of reincarnation, Li Fan had only heard of the Realm of Birth and Destruction once.

It came from the memory of the Taiyi Sacred Scripture, stolen from the Primordial Realm!

A long time ago, when that dark figure named "Zhu Kong," who stole thirty True Immortal Runes from the Taiyi Sacred Scripture, was being pursued by the Dao Comprehension Immortal, he was from the Realm of Birth and Destruction.

Besides Zhu Kong, there were four others. Together, they resisted the attack of the Dao Comprehension Immortal.

The Dao Comprehension Immortal called them the "Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation."

In order to retrieve the stolen thirty True Immortal Runes, the Dao Comprehension Immortal pursued Zhu Kong but never returned.

The danger of the Realm of Birth and Destruction was evident.

Relying on the faint connection with the stolen True Immortal Runes from the Taiyi Sacred Scripture, Li Fan knew the general location of the Realm of Birth and Destruction.

Therefore, in his subsequent travels through the mountains and seas, he deliberately avoided it.

Now it seemed he had indeed evaded a calamity.

"After so many years, have these so-called Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation become this powerful?"

"That sense of crisis I felt just now..."

"Even if they are not Saints, they are not far from it."

"Of course, it is also possible that it is due to the combined efforts of the Five Sovereigns."

"I wonder how the Faceless Immortal managed to provoke such a formidable foe?" Li Fan's mind raced with countless thoughts.

At the same time, he felt relieved.

The mountains and seas were still the same mountains and seas he knew. There was no suddenly appearing, fatal enemy.

It was just that the target he needed to be wary of had increased exponentially.

"Next, Fellow Daoist, what are your intentions?"

After experiencing such a major upheaval, and still holding onto Li Fan's promise of helping him become a Saint, Ho Gan Immortal looked at the contemplative Li Fan and asked.

Li Fan glanced at Ho Gan Immortal and said gravely, "I intend to go to the Realm of Birth and Destruction to investigate."

"Are you willing to accompany me?"

Chapter 1643: The Calamity of the Five Saints Reversing the Spirit

"Fellow Daoist, is what you say true?" Ho Gan Immortal froze, his expression showing clear hesitation.

"Heh, of course..."

"Not true," Li Fan's tone suddenly shifted.

"Although I have pursued them for a long time, their strength has undergone a qualitative change compared to before. Now, it seems, the Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation working together possess power close to that of a Saint, which I cannot currently match. We need to plan carefully..."

Li Fan's honest words surprised Ho Gan Immortal.

"Do you know what kind of beings these Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation are?" Li Fan asked.

Ho Gan Immortal shook his head.

"They are entirely different from the Saints of the Shore or the Saint Emperor Tai Wei. Their goal is not to save the Mountains and Seas, but like great daos that steal from a nation, they forcibly take rare items from various possibilities to fill themselves, aiming to create possibilities that rival the Mountains and Seas, or even transcend them and face Dao Annihilation," Li Fan gazed into the depths of the Mountains and Seas, his voice melancholic, as if he had personally visited the Realm of Birth and Destruction.

"How can this be allowed? If they steal a part, won't the Mountains and Seas lose a part of their foundation, and our chances of resisting Dao Annihilation decrease?" Ho Gan Immortal, being a cultivator of Virtue, was enraged upon hearing this.

"Furthermore, this action is almost equivalent to remaking the Mountains and Seas. Is it that easy? Not to mention that the five of them combined barely possess Saintly cultivation. Even if five true Saints worked together, they might not be able to achieve it. Otherwise, why would the Saints of the Shore endure hardships to traverse the Eternal Void?"

"Originally, I thought so too. I believed the Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation were merely engaging in meaningless actions, a death throe. But now..."

Li Fan suddenly thought of Emperor Tian Luo's successful plan to lead the Primordial Immortal Realm to transcendence, and the Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation personally visiting the Primordial Realm to steal True Immortal Seal Scripts from the Supreme Dao Scripture.

Judging by the expression of the Dao Comprehension Immortal at that time, the thirty stolen True Immortal Seal Scripts were certainly not simple.

"If my guess is correct, and the Primordial Immortal Realm truly transcended the Mountains and Seas, then it might not be impossible for the Realm of Birth and Destruction to repeat this story."

As this thought flashed through his mind, Li Fan continued, "Their strength has grown beyond my expectations. The Realm of Birth and Destruction is likely the same. This shows how much of the Mountains and Seas' foundation they have secretly stolen."

"Perhaps they are still lacking now. But if they continue like this..."

In Ho Gan Immortal's mind, an image appeared of a group of shadowy figures, silently roaming various parts of the Mountains and Seas, stealing resources.

An uncontrollable anger rose in his heart. If it weren't for the fact that he was truly no match for the Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation, he would have almost dragged Li Fan to investigate the Realm of Birth and Destruction himself.

Seeing Ho Gan Immortal's subtle shift in expression, Li Fan nodded inwardly, gaining a deeper understanding of his temperament. Smiling, he said, "Although the Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation are audacious, we have no need to worry excessively. After all, the Mountains and Seas are not ours alone, but the collective undertaking of all Immortals, Saints, and Saint Emperors."

"The Saints and Saint Emperors are fighting beyond the Eternal Void, in even more ancient timelines. How could they allow such parasites to exist in their rear?"

Ho Gan Immortal pondered, "Fellow Daoist, you mean..."

"We merely need to spread the news of the Realm of Birth and Destruction and then watch from the sidelines. Although the Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation are strong, how can they be stronger than the foundation of the Shore?"

Ho Gan Immortal felt somewhat awkward, "Due to the unique nature of my cultivation path, I have always been wary of the Shore and have never gone there. I only vaguely know the names of the Saints of the Shore."

"It's alright, I have my methods. Just watch, Fellow Daoist."

As he spoke, a figure appeared before Li Fan, exactly identical to his true form. Except for its distorted face, making its specific appearance unclear, its aura was indistinguishable from Li Fan's.

But in Ho Gan Immortal's eyes, it seemed like another independent entity.

"This avatar technique of Fellow Daoist..." The more he observed and pondered, the more shocked Ho Gan Immortal became. This clone he created was almost identical to the 'other self' generated through his method of separating Dao and Virtue. However, his was solely reliant on the special nature of the Great Dao of Virtue, and even after separation, Dao and Virtue still had intertwined connections. For example, just now, he could use the same method to sense the memories of his other self before its demise.

And Ho Gan Immortal could sense that the clone created by Li Fan's divine ability of avatar cultivation... seemed to be a completely independent individual.

Almost instantly, Ho Gan Immortal understood what Li Fan was about to do. Just as he thought, the newly created Li Fan received his mission, and intense flames erupted around his body. After a moment of brewing, he transformed into a streak of light and vanished into the Mountains and Seas.

The True Spirit Great Fire, which had not yet subsided, was reignited. This time, the new Li Fan was even more unbridled, rapidly traversing through the Dao Annihilation and igniting every possibility it passed.

"Fellow Daoist, you this..." Ho Gan Immortal's eyelids twitched as he watched.

Li Fan merely smiled and waved his hand. If the previous True Spirit Great Fire that isolated the Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation was limited to a corner of the Mountains and Seas, then the disaster Li Fan was now creating had the potential to spread across a vast area. Even if left to burn, the early demise of this segment of Mountains and Seas was not impossible.

Knowing that the Shore would inevitably respond, Li Fan calculated the time and decisively dispelled a wisp of his divine consciousness left on the avatar. This divine consciousness was not for communicating with the avatar, but as a catalyst for using the True Spirit Annihilation technique.

Li Fan's avatar divine ability, which comprehended the transformation of true and false, was profound, but it had not yet reached the point where it could create infinite spirituality. He could not even allocate his own infinite spirituality. Therefore, to ignite the Mountains and Seas, he still needed to act personally. Now that a significant commotion had been made, he burned the residual divine consciousness, destroying the only clue that could track his true form. The avatar, according to plan, then fled rapidly towards the location of the Realm of Birth and Destruction.

"Although this move can expose the Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation, the price is also a bit too high!" Ho Gan Immortal said somewhat regretfully, looking at the Mountains and Seas, which were now a sea of flames as far as the eye could see.

"To treat a serious illness, one needs drastic medicine. If this can eliminate the Five Sovereigns of the Same Tribulation in one fell swoop, this price is absolutely worth it. Don't worry, this possibility is insignificant compared to the final outcome."

"Look, if not for this, how could the Shore have gone to such great lengths?"

Ho Gan Immortal saw Li Fan point his finger, but for some reason, his eyes were closed. Thinking that the other party was afraid of exposure after setting the fire, he didn't probe further.
